                NEX_ESPState.SurvivorESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "Survivor ESP")
                NEX_ESPState.KillerESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "Killer ESP")
                NEX_ESPState.SpectatorESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "Spectator ESP")
                NEX_ESPState.SurvivorItemsESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "Survivor Items ESP")

                if NEX_ESPState.PlayerMasterESP then
                    NEX_StartPlayerLoop()
                    NEX_RefreshAllPlayers()
                else
                    NEX_ClearAllPlayerESP()
                end
            end,
        })

        playerSection:AddToggle({
            Name = "Player Nametags",
            Flag = "NEX Player Nametags",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(state)
                NEX_ESPState.Nametags = state
                if NEX_ESPState.PlayerMasterESP then
                    NEX_StartPlayerLoop()
                    NEX_RefreshAllPlayers()
                else
                    NEX_ClearAllPlayerESP()
                end
            end,
        })

        playerSection:AddToggle({
            Name = "Player Distance ESP",
            Flag = "NEX Player Distance ESP",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(state)
                NEX_ESPState.DistanceESP = state
                if NEX_ESPState.PlayerMasterESP then
                    NEX_StartPlayerLoop()
                    NEX_RefreshAllPlayers()
                else
                    NEX_ClearAllPlayerESP()
                end
            end,
        })

        playerSection:AddToggle({
            Name = "Trapped & Chained Status ESP",
            Flag = "NEX Trapped & Chained ESP",
            Default = true,
            Callback = function(state)
                NEX_ESPState.TrappedChainedESP = state
                if NEX_ESPState.PlayerMasterESP then
                    NEX_RefreshAllPlayers()
                end
            end,
        })

        playerSection:AddToggle({
            Name = "Survivor Killer Warning (!)",
            Flag = "Survivor Killer Warning",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(state)
                VD.SURV_WarnKiller = state
            end,
        })

        pcall(function() playerSection:AddDivider({ Text = "Colors" }) end)
        playerSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Survivor Color", Flag = "NEX Survivor Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.SurvivorColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.SurvivorColor = color; NEX_RefreshAllPlayers() end })
        playerSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Killer Color", Flag = "NEX Killer Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.KillerColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.KillerColor = color; NEX_RefreshAllPlayers() end })
        playerSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Spectator Color", Flag = "NEX Spectator Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.SpectatorColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.SpectatorColor = color; NEX_RefreshAllPlayers() end })
        playerSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Trapped Survivor Color", Flag = "NEX Trapped Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.TrappedColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.TrappedColor = color; NEX_RefreshAllPlayers() end })
        playerSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Chained Survivor Color", Flag = "NEX Chained Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.ChainedColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.ChainedColor = color; NEX_RefreshAllPlayers() end })

        local worldSection = VisualTabRef:AddSection({
            Position = "Center",
            Name = "World Highlight ESP",
            Icon = "solar:map-point-wave-bold",
            Box = true,
            BoxBorder = true,
            Opened = false,
        })

        worldSection:AddToggle({
            Name = "Enable World ESP",
            Flag = "NEX Enable World ESP",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(state)
                NEX_ESPState.WorldMasterESP = state
                if state then
                    NEX_RefreshESPRoots()
                    if NEX_AnyWorldEnabled() then NEX_StartWorldLoop() end
                else
                    NEX_ClearAllWorldESP()
                end
            end,
        })

        worldSection:AddDropdown({
            Name = "Select World Objects",
            Flag = "NEX Select World Objects",
            Values = { "Generators", "Hooks", "Gates", "Windows", "Pallets", "SCP / Zombie", "Butcher Traps", "Halloween Pumpkins" },
            Multi = true,
            AllowNone = true,
            Default = {},
            Callback = function(selected)
                NEX_ESPState.GeneratorESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "Generators")
                NEX_ESPState.HookESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "Hooks")
                NEX_ESPState.GateESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "Gates")
                NEX_ESPState.WindowESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "Windows")
                NEX_ESPState.PalletESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "Pallets")
                NEX_ESPState.SCPZombieESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "SCP / Zombie")
                NEX_ESPState.ButcherTrapESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "Butcher Traps")
                NEX_ESPState.PumpkinESP = NEX_Selected(selected, "Halloween Pumpkins")

                if NEX_ESPState.WorldMasterESP and NEX_AnyWorldEnabled() then
                    NEX_RefreshESPRoots()
                    NEX_StartWorldLoop()
                else
                    NEX_ClearAllWorldESP()
                end
            end,
        })

        worldSection:AddToggle({
            Name = "World Nametags",
            Flag = "NEX World Nametags",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(state)
                NEX_ESPState.WorldNametags = state
                if NEX_ESPState.WorldMasterESP and NEX_AnyWorldEnabled() then NEX_StartWorldLoop() else NEX_ClearAllWorldESP() end
            end,
        })

        worldSection:AddToggle({
            Name = "World Distance ESP",
            Flag = "NEX World Distance ESP",
            Default = false,
            Callback = function(state)
                NEX_ESPState.WorldDistanceESP = state
                if NEX_ESPState.WorldMasterESP and NEX_AnyWorldEnabled() then NEX_StartWorldLoop() else NEX_ClearAllWorldESP() end
            end,
        })

        pcall(function() worldSection:AddDivider({ Text = "Colors" }) end)
        worldSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Generator Color", Flag = "NEX Generator Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.GeneratorColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.GeneratorColor = color end })
        worldSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Hook Color", Flag = "NEX Hook Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.HookColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.HookColor = color end })
        worldSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Gate Color", Flag = "NEX Gate Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.GateColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.GateColor = color end })
        worldSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Window Color", Flag = "NEX Window Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.WindowColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.WindowColor = color end })
        worldSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Pallet Color", Flag = "NEX Pallet Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.PalletColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.PalletColor = color end })
        worldSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "SCP / Zombie Color", Flag = "NEX SCP Zombie Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.SCPZombieColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.SCPZombieColor = color end })
        worldSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Butcher Trap Color", Flag = "NEX Butcher Trap Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.ButcherTrapColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.ButcherTrapColor = color end })
        worldSection:AddColorPicker({ Name = "Halloween Pumpkin Color", Flag = "NEX Halloween Pumpkin Color", Default = NEX_ESPState.PumpkinColor, Callback = function(color) NEX_ESPState.PumpkinColor = color end })
    end

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        NEX_WatchPlayer(player)
    end

    table.insert(NEX_Connections, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(NEX_WatchPlayer))
    table.insert(NEX_Connections, Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(NEX_UnwatchPlayer))
    table.insert(NEX_Connections, Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "Map" or child.Name == "Map1" then
            NEX_AttachESPRoot(child)
            if NEX_ESPState.WorldMasterESP and NEX_AnyWorldEnabled() then NEX_StartWorldLoop() end
        end
    end))
    table.insert(NEX_Connections, Workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "Map" or child.Name == "Map1" then
            NEX_RefreshESPRoots()
        end
    end))
    table.insert(NEX_Connections, CollectionService:GetInstanceAddedSignal("BearTrap"):Connect(function(inst)
        local m = inst:IsA("Model") and inst or (inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or inst)
        NEX_EnsureWorldEntry("ButcherTrap", m)
    end))
    table.insert(NEX_Connections, CollectionService:GetInstanceRemovedSignal("BearTrap"):Connect(function(inst)
        local m = inst:IsA("Model") and inst or (inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or inst)
        NEX_RemoveWorldEntry("ButcherTrap", m)
    end))
    table.insert(NEX_Connections, CollectionService:GetInstanceAddedSignal("pumpkin"):Connect(function(inst)
        local m = inst:IsA("Model") and inst or (inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or inst)
        NEX_EnsureWorldEntry("Pumpkin", m)
    end))
    table.insert(NEX_Connections, CollectionService:GetInstanceRemovedSignal("pumpkin"):Connect(function(inst)
        local m = inst:IsA("Model") and inst or (inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or inst)
        NEX_RemoveWorldEntry("Pumpkin", m)
    end))
    table.insert(NEX_Connections, CollectionService:GetInstanceAddedSignal("Pumpkin"):Connect(function(inst)
        local m = inst:IsA("Model") and inst or (inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or inst)
        NEX_EnsureWorldEntry("Pumpkin", m)
    end))
    table.insert(NEX_Connections, CollectionService:GetInstanceRemovedSignal("Pumpkin"):Connect(function(inst)
        local m = inst:IsA("Model") and inst or (inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or inst)
        NEX_RemoveWorldEntry("Pumpkin", m)
    end))

    NEX_RefreshESPRoots()

    getgenv().NEX_VD_VisualESP_Cleanup = function()
        NEX_Dead = true
        NEX_ClearAllPlayerESP()
        NEX_ClearAllWorldESP()

        for _, conn in ipairs(NEX_Connections) do
            if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
        end
        for _, conns in pairs(NEX_PlayerConns) do
            for _, conn in ipairs(conns) do
                if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
            end
        end
        for _, conn in pairs(NEX_MapAdd) do
            if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
        end
        for _, conn in pairs(NEX_MapRem) do
            if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
        end
        if NEX_ESPFolder and NEX_ESPFolder.Parent then
            NEX_ESPFolder:Destroy()
        end
    end

    NEX_SafeNotify("Visual ESP", "Highlight ESP V2 loaded. Anti double nametag aktif.", 3)
end



-- =====================================================
-- FULLBRIGHT
-- =====================================================
task.spawn(function()
    while not VD.Destroyed do
        if VD.Fullbright then
            local weatherTheme = VD.VIS_WeatherTheme and NEX_WeatherPresets[VD.VIS_WeatherTheme]
            local keepWeatherLighting = VD.VIS_WeatherTheme and VD.VIS_WeatherTheme ~= "Default" and weatherTheme and weatherTheme.Lighting
            if keepWeatherLighting then
                for k, v in pairs(weatherTheme.Lighting) do
                    pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end)
                end
                Lighting.Brightness = math.max(Lighting.Brightness, 2)
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
                if VD.NO_Fog then
                    Lighting.FogStart = 0
                    Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
                end
            else
                Lighting.Brightness     = 2
                Lighting.ClockTime      = 14
                Lighting.GlobalShadows  = false
                Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
                Lighting.FogStart       = 0
                Lighting.FogEnd         = 100000
            end
            for _, v in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                if v:IsA("Atmosphere") and v.Name ~= "VD_WeatherAtmosphere" then
                    v.Density = 0; v.Offset = 0; v.Glare = 0; v.Haze = 0
                end
                if v:IsA("BlurEffect") then v.Size = 0 end
                if v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") and v.Name ~= "VD_WeatherCC" then v.Enabled = false end
                if v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") then v.Enabled = false end
            end
        else
            if VD.VIS_WeatherTheme and VD.VIS_WeatherTheme ~= "Default" and NEX_WeatherPresets[VD.VIS_WeatherTheme] then
                local theme = NEX_WeatherPresets[VD.VIS_WeatherTheme]
                if theme.Lighting then
                    for k, v in pairs(theme.Lighting) do
                        pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end)
                    end
                end
            else
                Lighting.Brightness     = originalLighting.Brightness
                Lighting.ClockTime      = originalLighting.ClockTime
                Lighting.FogEnd         = originalLighting.FogEnd
                Lighting.FogStart       = originalLighting.FogStart or 0
                Lighting.GlobalShadows  = originalLighting.GlobalShadows
                Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = originalLighting.OutdoorAmbient
                for _, v in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:IsA("Atmosphere") and originalLighting.Atmosphere then
                        v.Density = originalLighting.Atmosphere.Density or 0.3
                        v.Offset  = originalLighting.Atmosphere.Offset or 0.25
                        v.Glare   = originalLighting.Atmosphere.Glare or 0
                        v.Haze    = originalLighting.Atmosphere.Haze or 0
                    end
                    if v:IsA("BlurEffect") and originalLighting.Blur then v.Size = originalLighting.Blur.Size or 0 end
                    if v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") and originalLighting.ColorCorrection then
                        v.Enabled = originalLighting
                            .ColorCorrection.Enabled or false
                    end
                    if v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") and originalLighting.SunRays then
                        v.Enabled = originalLighting.SunRays.Enabled or
                            false
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.5)
    end
end)

local cachedPlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if VD.HideSkillUI then
        if not cachedPlayerGui then cachedPlayerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") end
        local a = cachedPlayerGui and cachedPlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SkillCheckPromptGui")
        local b = cachedPlayerGui and cachedPlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SkillCheckPromptGui-con")
        if a and a.Enabled then a.Enabled = false end
        if b and b.Enabled then b.Enabled = false end
    end
end)

-- =====================================================
-- AUTO PARRY + AUTO SKILLCHECK (ported from survivor)
-- =====================================================
function VD_Notify(title, content, duration)
    pcall(function()
        if Window and Window.Notify then
            Window:Notify({
                Title = title,
                Content = content,
                Duration = duration or 2,
                Icon = "lucide:info",
            })
        end
    end)
end

-- =====================================================
-- SILENT AIM: TWIST OF FATE
-- =====================================================
(function()
local NEX_ToFState = {
    Connection = nil,
    LaserBeam = nil,
    TargetGui = nil,
    InputBegan = nil,
    InputEnded = nil,
    TouchInput = nil,
    IsAiming = false,
    LastHookedGun = nil,
    SavedUIPos = UDim2.new(0.5, -120, 0, 110),
    SCPCache = {},
    SCPCacheTimer = 0,
}

local NEX_ToFKeyCodes = {
    None = nil,
    Q = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
    E = Enum.KeyCode.E,
    R = Enum.KeyCode.R,
    T = Enum.KeyCode.T,
    F = Enum.KeyCode.F,
    G = Enum.KeyCode.G,
    H = Enum.KeyCode.H,
    J = Enum.KeyCode.J,
    K = Enum.KeyCode.K,
    L = Enum.KeyCode.L,
    X = Enum.KeyCode.X,
    Z = Enum.KeyCode.Z,
}

local function NEX_ToFResolveKey(keyStr)
    if not keyStr or keyStr == "" or keyStr == "None" then return nil end
    if NEX_ToFKeyCodes[keyStr] then return NEX_ToFKeyCodes[keyStr] end
    local ok, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[keyStr] end)
    return (ok and kc) or nil
end

local function NEX_ToFGetEvent()
    local cached = getgenv().NEX_ToFFireRemote
    if cached and cached.Parent then
        return cached
    end
    local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local items = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Items")
    local tof = items and items:FindFirstChild("Twist of Fate")
    local fire = tof and tof:FindFirstChild("Fire")
    if fire and fire:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        getgenv().NEX_ToFFireRemote = fire
        return fire
    end
    return nil
end
NEX_ToFGetEvent()

local function NEX_ToFGetGunObject()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return nil end

    local baseToF = char:FindFirstChild("Twist of Fate") or char:FindFirstChild("Twist of Fate", true)
    if not baseToF then return nil end

    return baseToF
end

-- Hitung titik keluar peluru sesuai rumus safeOrigin di TwistoffateEffects.own
local function NEX_ToFGetBulletOrigin(char, hrp, baseCF)
    local cf = baseCF or hrp.CFrame
    if char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then
        return hrp.Position + (cf.LookVector * 2)
    end
    local p49 = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.373, 0)
    local p50 = (cf * CFrame.new(0.7, 1.373, -2.5)).Position
    local v51 = p50 - p49
    if v51.Magnitude <= 0 then
        return p50
    end
    local ok, res = pcall(function()
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
        params.IgnoreWater = true
        local hit = workspace:Blockcast(CFrame.new(p49, p50), Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), v51, params)
        if hit then
            return hit.Position - v51.Unit * 0.5
        end
        return p50
    end)
    return (ok and res) or p50
end

local function NEX_ToFIsTargetVisible(originPos, targetPos, targetCharacter)
    local direction = targetPos - originPos
    local distance = direction.Magnitude
    if distance < 0.1 then return true end

    local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    rayParams.IgnoreWater = true

    local excludeList = {}
    local localChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    if localChar then table.insert(excludeList, localChar) end
    if targetCharacter and targetCharacter ~= localChar then table.insert(excludeList, targetCharacter) end
    if NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam then table.insert(excludeList, NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam) end
    rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = excludeList

    local curOrigin = originPos
    local curDir = direction.Unit * distance
    for _ = 1, 10 do
        if curDir.Magnitude <= 0.05 then return true end
        local result = workspace:Raycast(curOrigin, curDir, rayParams)
        if not result then return true end

        local hitInst = result.Instance
        local model = hitInst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        local isSurvivor = false
        if model and model ~= targetCharacter then
            local p = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
            if (p and p.Team and p.Team.Name == "Survivors") or CollectionService:HasTag(model, "Survivor") or CollectionService:HasTag(model, "SurvivorBot") then
                isSurvivor = true
            end
        end
        local isAccessory = hitInst:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Accessory") ~= nil
        local isPassable = (not hitInst.CanCollide) or (hitInst.Transparency > 0.01 and hitInst.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart")

        if not (isSurvivor or isAccessory or isPassable) then
            return false
        end

        table.insert(excludeList, (isSurvivor and model) or hitInst)
        rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = excludeList
        local rem = curDir.Magnitude - (result.Position - curOrigin).Magnitude
        if rem <= 0.05 then return true end
        curOrigin = result.Position + curDir.Unit * 0.01
        curDir = curDir.Unit * rem
    end
    return true
end

local AimConfig = {
    Pistol_BlockKnocked = true,
}

local function IsDowned(char)
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return true end
    local state = char:GetAttribute("State")
    if state == "Downed" or state == "Dead" then return true end
    if char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") or char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then return true end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and hum.Health < hum.MaxHealth * 0.5 then return true end
    return false
end

local function NEX_ToFCanUseLogic()
    if not VD.TOF_SilentAim then return false end
    AimConfig.Pistol_BlockKnocked = VD.TOF_BlockKnocked ~= false
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return false end
    if AimConfig.Pistol_BlockKnocked and IsDowned(char) then
        return false
    end
    return true
end

local function NEX_ToFGetSCPs()
    if tick() - NEX_ToFState.SCPCacheTimer < 0.5 then
        return NEX_ToFState.SCPCache
    end

    local newTargets = {}
    local mapFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    if mapFolder then
        for _, container in pairs(mapFolder:GetDescendants()) do
            if container:IsA("Model") then
                local attributes = container:GetAttributes()
                if container:GetAttribute("CorpseCreated0492") or next(attributes) ~= nil then
                    local root = container:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if root then table.insert(newTargets, root) end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    NEX_ToFState.SCPCache = newTargets
    NEX_ToFState.SCPCacheTimer = tick()
    return NEX_ToFState.SCPCache
end

local function NEX_ToFGetTargetPosition()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return nil, nil, nil, nil, nil end

    local gunObj = NEX_ToFGetGunObject()
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return nil, nil, nil, nil, nil end

    local myPos = hrp.Position

    -- Hitung arah persis mengikuti garis Laser (dari muzzle saat menghadap target lurus ke torso target)
    local function computeLaserTrajectory(torso, targetCharacter)
        local targetPos = torso.Position

        local aimedHrpCF = hrp.CFrame
        if not char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then
            local flatTarget = Vector3.new(targetPos.X, myPos.Y, targetPos.Z)
            if (flatTarget - myPos).Magnitude > 0.001 then
                aimedHrpCF = CFrame.new(myPos, flatTarget)
            end
        end

        -- Hitung originPos menggunakan CFrame karakter yang sudah menghadap target
        -- agar titik awal peluru (v96) 100% identik dengan titik awal garis laser
        local originPos = NEX_ToFGetBulletOrigin(char, hrp, aimedHrpCF)

        if VD.TOF_WallCheck and not NEX_ToFIsTargetVisible(originPos, targetPos, targetCharacter) then
            return nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
        end

        local laserVec = targetPos - originPos
        if laserVec.Magnitude < 0.05 then
            return nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
        end

        return laserVec.Unit, gunObj, originPos, targetPos, aimedHrpCF
    end

    local targetMode = VD.TOF_TargetMode or "Killer"
    if targetMode ~= "Killer" and targetMode ~= "Zombie" then
        targetMode = "Killer"
        VD.TOF_TargetMode = "Killer"
    end

    if targetMode == "Killer" then
        local closestTorso, closestChar, shortestDist = nil, nil, math.huge
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Team and player.Team.Name == "Killer" and player.Character then
                local torso = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    or player.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                    or player.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                if torso then
                    local dist = (myPos - torso.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist < shortestDist then
                        shortestDist = dist
                        closestTorso = torso
                        closestChar = player.Character
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if not closestTorso then return nil, nil, nil, nil, nil end
        return computeLaserTrajectory(closestTorso, closestChar)
    elseif targetMode == "Zombie" then
        local bestPart, bestDot = nil, -math.huge
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local camLook = cam.CFrame.LookVector

        for _, root in ipairs(NEX_ToFGetSCPs()) do
            if root and root.Parent then
                local dirToTarget = root.Position - cam.CFrame.Position
                if dirToTarget.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                    local dot = camLook:Dot(dirToTarget.Unit)
                    if dot > 0.5 and dot > bestDot then
                        bestDot = dot
                        bestPart = root
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if not bestPart then return nil, nil, nil, nil, nil end
        return computeLaserTrajectory(bestPart, bestPart.Parent)
    end

    return nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
end

local function NEX_ToFApplyCharacterAim(char, aimedHrpCF)
    if not (char and aimedHrpCF) or char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp then
        hrp.CFrame = aimedHrpCF
    end
    local animCtrl = NEX_ToFState.AnimController
    if type(animCtrl) == "table" and rawget(animCtrl, "humanoidRootPart") == hrp then
        local look = aimedHrpCF.LookVector
        local yaw = math.atan2(-look.X, -look.Z)
        rawset(animCtrl, "currentYaw", yaw)
        rawset(animCtrl, "targetYaw", yaw)
        rawset(animCtrl, "lastYaw", yaw)
        rawset(animCtrl, "accumulatedRotation", 0)
    end
end

local function NEX_ToFHookEffectsTable(Effects)
    if type(Effects) ~= "table" then return end
    if not rawget(Effects, "_nexToFAimDirOrig") and type(rawget(Effects, "aimDirection")) == "function" then
        rawset(Effects, "_nexToFAimDirOrig", rawget(Effects, "aimDirection"))
    end
    if not rawget(Effects, "_nexToFOwnOrig") and type(rawget(Effects, "own")) == "function" then
        rawset(Effects, "_nexToFOwnOrig", rawget(Effects, "own"))
    end

    local origAimDirection = rawget(Effects, "_nexToFAimDirOrig")
    if type(origAimDirection) == "function" then
        rawset(Effects, "aimDirection", function(pChar, pCamCF, ...)
            if NEX_ToFCanUseLogic() and pChar == LocalPlayer.Character then
                local targetDir, _, _, _, aimedHrpCF = NEX_ToFGetTargetPosition()
                if targetDir then
                    NEX_ToFApplyCharacterAim(pChar, aimedHrpCF)
                    getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedDir = targetDir
                    return targetDir
                end
            end
            return origAimDirection(pChar, pCamCF, ...)
        end)
    end

    local origOwn = rawget(Effects, "_nexToFOwnOrig")
    if type(origOwn) == "function" then
        rawset(Effects, "own", function(pChar, pGun, pDir, ...)
            if NEX_ToFCanUseLogic() and pChar == LocalPlayer.Character then
                local targetDir, _, _, _, aimedHrpCF = NEX_ToFGetTargetPosition()
                if targetDir then
                    NEX_ToFApplyCharacterAim(pChar, aimedHrpCF)
                    pDir = targetDir
                    getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedDir = targetDir
                elseif getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedDir then
                    pDir = getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedDir
                end
            end
            return origOwn(pChar, pGun, pDir, ...)
        end)
    end
end

local function NEX_ToFHookFacingTable(Facing)
    if type(Facing) ~= "table" then return end
    if not rawget(Facing, "_nexToFReqOrig") and type(rawget(Facing, "request")) == "function" then
        rawset(Facing, "_nexToFReqOrig", rawget(Facing, "request"))
    end
    if not rawget(Facing, "_nexToFGetOrig") and type(rawget(Facing, "get")) == "function" then
        rawset(Facing, "_nexToFGetOrig", rawget(Facing, "get"))
    end

    local origRequest = rawget(Facing, "_nexToFReqOrig")
    if type(origRequest) == "function" then
        rawset(Facing, "request", function(pRoot, pDir, ...)
            if NEX_ToFCanUseLogic() then
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                local isAimingNow = NEX_ToFState.IsAiming or (char and char:GetAttribute("Aiming") == true)
                if char and isAimingNow and pRoot == char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local flatDir = getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedFlatDir or getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedDir
                    if flatDir then
                        pDir = flatDir
                    end
                end
            end
            return origRequest(pRoot, pDir, ...)
        end)
    end

    local origGet = rawget(Facing, "_nexToFGetOrig")
    if type(origGet) == "function" then
        rawset(Facing, "get", function(pRoot, ...)
            if NEX_ToFCanUseLogic() then
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                local isAimingNow = NEX_ToFState.IsAiming or (char and char:GetAttribute("Aiming") == true)
                if char and isAimingNow and pRoot == char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local flatDir = getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedFlatDir
                    if flatDir then
                        return flatDir
                    end
                end
            end
            return origGet(pRoot, ...)
        end)
    end
end

-- Scan getgc(true) + getrenv().require + require agar tabel asli di memori LocalScript game ikut ter-hook di Delta
local function NEX_ToFHookAllModules()
    pcall(function()
        local modules = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
        local items = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Items")
        local tofFolder = items and items:FindFirstChild("TwistOfFate")
        local effectsMod = tofFolder and tofFolder:FindFirstChild("TwistoffateEffects")
        if effectsMod then
            pcall(function() NEX_ToFHookEffectsTable(require(effectsMod)) end)
            if type(getrenv) == "function" and getrenv().require then
                pcall(function() NEX_ToFHookEffectsTable(getrenv().require(effectsMod)) end)
            end
        end

        local survFolder = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Survivors")
        local facingMod = survFolder and survFolder:FindFirstChild("SurvivorFacing")
        if facingMod then
            pcall(function() NEX_ToFHookFacingTable(require(facingMod)) end)
            if type(getrenv) == "function" and getrenv().require then
                pcall(function() NEX_ToFHookFacingTable(getrenv().require(facingMod)) end)
            end
        end

        if type(getgc) == "function" then
            local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            for _, obj in ipairs(getgc(true)) do
                if type(obj) == "table" then
                    if type(rawget(obj, "aimDirection")) == "function" and type(rawget(obj, "own")) == "function" and type(rawget(obj, "bullet")) == "function" then
                        NEX_ToFHookEffectsTable(obj)
                    elseif type(rawget(obj, "request")) == "function" and type(rawget(obj, "get")) == "function" and rawget(obj, "aimDirection") == nil then
                        NEX_ToFHookFacingTable(obj)
                    elseif myHrp and rawget(obj, "humanoidRootPart") == myHrp and rawget(obj, "TURN_ENABLED") ~= nil then
                        NEX_ToFState.AnimController = obj
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.5)
    NEX_ToFHookAllModules()
end)

-- Cache visibility state & arah peluru setiap frame di luar __namecall
getgenv().NEX_TOF_WallBlocked = true
getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedDir = nil
getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedFlatDir = nil
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    NEX_ToFGetEvent()
    local gunObj = NEX_ToFGetGunObject()
    if gunObj and gunObj ~= NEX_ToFState.LastHookedGun then
        NEX_ToFState.LastHookedGun = gunObj
        task.spawn(NEX_ToFHookAllModules)
    end

    if not NEX_ToFCanUseLogic() then
        getgenv().NEX_TOF_WallBlocked = false
        getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedDir = nil
        getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedFlatDir = nil
        return
    end
    local targetDir, _, _, _, aimedHrpCF = NEX_ToFGetTargetPosition()
    getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedDir = targetDir
    getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedFlatDir = (aimedHrpCF and aimedHrpCF.LookVector) or nil
    if VD.TOF_WallCheck then
        getgenv().NEX_TOF_WallBlocked = (targetDir == nil)
    else
        getgenv().NEX_TOF_WallBlocked = false
    end
end)

local function NEX_ToFUpdateLaser(originPos, targetPos)
    if not NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam then
        local laser = Instance.new("Part")
        laser.Name = "ToFLaser"
        laser.Anchored = true
        laser.CanCollide = false
        laser.CanTouch = false
        laser.CanQuery = false
        laser.CastShadow = false
        laser.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        laser.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
        laser.Parent = workspace
        NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam = laser
    end

    local dist = (targetPos - originPos).Magnitude
    NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam.Size = Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, dist)
    NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam.CFrame = CFrame.new((originPos + targetPos) / 2, targetPos)
    NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam.Transparency = 0
end

local function NEX_ToFClearLaser()
    if NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam then
        pcall(function() NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam:Destroy() end)
        NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam = nil
    end
end

local function NEX_ToFGetMobileShootButton()
    local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local survivorMob = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob")
    local controls = survivorMob and survivorMob:FindFirstChild("Controls")
    local guiMob = controls and controls:FindFirstChild("Gui-mob")
    if not guiMob then return nil end
    return guiMob:IsA("GuiObject") and guiMob or nil
end

local function NEX_ToFIsTouchOnShootButton(input)
    local shootButton = NEX_ToFGetMobileShootButton()
    if not (shootButton and shootButton.Visible) then return false end

    local pos = input.Position
    local absPos = shootButton.AbsolutePosition
    local absSize = shootButton.AbsoluteSize

    return pos.X >= absPos.X and pos.X <= absPos.X + absSize.X
        and pos.Y >= absPos.Y and pos.Y <= absPos.Y + absSize.Y
end

local NEX_ToFModeButtons = {}
local function NEX_ToFRefreshTargetButtons()
    local modes = {
        Killer = { Color3.fromRGB(180, 45, 45), Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 180) },
        Zombie = { Color3.fromRGB(120, 80, 10), Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 100) },
    }

    for modeName, btn in pairs(NEX_ToFModeButtons) do
        if btn and btn.Parent then
            local active = modeName == (VD.TOF_TargetMode or "Killer")
            local colors = modes[modeName]
            if colors then
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = active and colors[1] or Color3.fromRGB(30, 32, 40)
                btn.TextColor3 = active and colors[2] or Color3.fromRGB(155, 160, 175)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function NEX_ToFSetTargetMode(modeName, notify)
    if modeName ~= "Killer" and modeName ~= "Zombie" then
        modeName = "Killer"
    end
    VD.TOF_TargetMode = modeName
    NEX_ToFRefreshTargetButtons()
    if notify then VD_Notify("Target Mode", modeName, 1) end
end

local function NEX_ToFCreateTargetSelectorUI()
    local parent = GetSafeGuiParent()
    if not parent then return end
    if NEX_ToFState.TargetGui and NEX_ToFState.TargetGui.Parent then return end

    local old = parent:FindFirstChild("ToFTargetSelector")
    if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "ToFTargetSelector"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.Parent = parent

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = "Main"
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 180, 0, 96)
    frame.Position = NEX_ToFState.SavedUIPos
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 18, 24)
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Active = true
    frame.Parent = gui
    Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(96, 72, 160)
    stroke.Thickness = 1

    local header = Instance.new("Frame")
    header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
    header.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 26, 34)
    header.BorderSizePixel = 0
    header.Parent = frame
    Instance.new("UICorner", header).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    local headerFix = Instance.new("Frame")
    headerFix.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10)
    headerFix.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -10)
    headerFix.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 26, 34)
    headerFix.BorderSizePixel = 0
    headerFix.Parent = header

    local headerDiv = Instance.new("Frame")
    headerDiv.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
    headerDiv.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1)
    headerDiv.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(48, 42, 72)
    headerDiv.BorderSizePixel = 0
    headerDiv.Parent = header

    local dragArea = Instance.new("Frame")
    dragArea.Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 1, 0)
    dragArea.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    dragArea.Parent = header

    local minimizeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    minimizeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 1, 0)
    minimizeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 0)
    minimizeBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    minimizeBtn.Text = "-"
    minimizeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(185, 190, 205)
    minimizeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    minimizeBtn.TextSize = 14
    minimizeBtn.Parent = header

    local headerLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    headerLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 1, 0)
    headerLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    headerLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    headerLbl.Text = "TOF TARGET MODE"
    headerLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 215, 230)
    headerLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    headerLbl.TextSize = 10
    headerLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    headerLbl.Parent = header

    local btnContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    btnContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 56)
    btnContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 34)
    btnContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btnContainer.Parent = frame

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", btnContainer)
    layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)

    local isMinimized = false
    minimizeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        isMinimized = not isMinimized
        minimizeBtn.Text = isMinimized and "+" or "-"
        btnContainer.Visible = not isMinimized
        frame.Size = isMinimized and UDim2.new(0, 180, 0, 28) or UDim2.new(0, 180, 0, 96)
    end)

    local modes = {
        { Internal = "Killer", Display = "KILLER        K" },
        { Internal = "Zombie", Display = "ZOMBIE        L" },
    }

    NEX_ToFModeButtons = {}
    for i, mode in ipairs(modes) do
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 25)
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        btn.TextSize = 11
        btn.Text = mode.Display
        btn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        btn.LayoutOrder = i
        btn.Parent = btnContainer
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

        local btnStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
        btnStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(58, 62, 78)
        btnStroke.Thickness = 1

        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            NEX_ToFSetTargetMode(mode.Internal, false)
        end)
        btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                NEX_ToFSetTargetMode(mode.Internal, false)
            end
        end)

        NEX_ToFModeButtons[mode.Internal] = btn
    end
    NEX_ToFRefreshTargetButtons()

    local dragging = false
    local dragStart, startPos
    dragArea.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = frame.Position
            dragging = true
        end
    end)
    dragArea.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            local newPos = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale,
                startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale,
                startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
            frame.Position = newPos
            NEX_ToFState.SavedUIPos = newPos
        end
    end)

    NEX_ToFState.TargetGui = gui
end

local function NEX_ToFDestroyTargetSelectorUI()
    if NEX_ToFState.TargetGui then
        pcall(function() NEX_ToFState.TargetGui:Destroy() end)
        NEX_ToFState.TargetGui = nil
    end
    NEX_ToFModeButtons = {}
end

local function NEX_ToFStepAimAndLaser()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local isAimingNow = NEX_ToFState.IsAiming or (char and char:GetAttribute("Aiming") == true)
    if not VD.TOF_SilentAim or not isAimingNow then
        if NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam then NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam.Transparency = 1 end
        return
    end

    local targetDir, _, originPos, targetPos, aimedHrpCF = NEX_ToFGetTargetPosition()
    if targetDir and originPos and targetPos then
        -- Rotasi karakter & arah tembakan SELALU mengikuti garis laser,
        -- baik saat visual ToF Laser dinyalakan maupun dimatikan
        pcall(NEX_ToFApplyCharacterAim, char, aimedHrpCF)
        getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedDir = targetDir
        getgenv().NEX_TOF_CachedFlatDir = (aimedHrpCF and aimedHrpCF.LookVector) or nil

        if VD.TOF_Laser then
            NEX_ToFUpdateLaser(originPos, targetPos)
        elseif NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam then
            NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam.Transparency = 1
        end
    elseif NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam then
        NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam.Transparency = 1
    end
end

local function NEX_ToFStartConnection()
    if not NEX_ToFState.Connection then
        NEX_ToFState.Connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(NEX_ToFStepAimAndLaser)
    end
    if not NEX_ToFState.RenderConnection then
        NEX_ToFState.RenderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(NEX_ToFStepAimAndLaser)
    end
end

local function NEX_ToFStopConnection()
    if NEX_ToFState.Connection then
        pcall(function() NEX_ToFState.Connection:Disconnect() end)
        NEX_ToFState.Connection = nil
    end
    if NEX_ToFState.RenderConnection then
        pcall(function() NEX_ToFState.RenderConnection:Disconnect() end)
        NEX_ToFState.RenderConnection = nil
    end
    NEX_ToFState.IsAiming = false
    NEX_ToFClearLaser()
end

local function NEX_ToFDisconnectInputs()
    if NEX_ToFState.InputBegan then pcall(function() NEX_ToFState.InputBegan:Disconnect() end) end
    if NEX_ToFState.InputEnded then pcall(function() NEX_ToFState.InputEnded:Disconnect() end) end
    NEX_ToFState.InputBegan = nil
    NEX_ToFState.InputEnded = nil
end

local NEX_SetToFSilentAim

local function NEX_ToFEnsureInputs()
    if not NEX_ToFState.InputBegan then
        NEX_ToFState.InputBegan = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
            if gameProcessed then return end

            local keyCode = NEX_ToFResolveKey(VD.TOF_Key)
            if keyCode and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode == keyCode then
                NEX_SetToFSilentAim(not VD.TOF_SilentAim)
                return
            end

            if not VD.TOF_SilentAim then return end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and NEX_ToFIsTouchOnShootButton(input)) then
                NEX_ToFState.IsAiming = true
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    NEX_ToFState.TouchInput = input
                end
                return
            end

            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.K then
                    NEX_ToFSetTargetMode("Killer", true)
                elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.L then
                    NEX_ToFSetTargetMode("Zombie", true)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    if not NEX_ToFState.InputEnded then
        NEX_ToFState.InputEnded = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == NEX_ToFState.TouchInput) then
                NEX_ToFState.IsAiming = false
                if input == NEX_ToFState.TouchInput then NEX_ToFState.TouchInput = nil end
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                if not (char and char:GetAttribute("Aiming") == true) then
                    if NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam then NEX_ToFState.LaserBeam.Transparency = 1 end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

NEX_SetToFSilentAim = function(enabled)
    VD.TOF_SilentAim = enabled and true or false
    NEX_ToFEnsureInputs()
    if VD.TOF_SilentAim then
        task.spawn(NEX_ToFHookAllModules)
        NEX_ToFCreateTargetSelectorUI()
        NEX_ToFStartConnection()
    else
        NEX_ToFDestroyTargetSelectorUI()
        NEX_ToFStopConnection()
    end
end

NEX_ToFEnsureInputs()
getgenv().NEX_SetToFSilentAim = NEX_SetToFSilentAim
getgenv().NEX_ToFClearLaser = NEX_ToFClearLaser
getgenv().NEX_ToFSetTargetMode = NEX_ToFSetTargetMode
end)();
-- =====================================================
-- HIDE SURVIVOR ICON
-- =====================================================
(function()
local NEX_HideSurvivorIconState = {
    Connection = nil,
    Originals = {},
}

local NEX_HideSurvivorIconImage = "rbxassetid://82006436469351"
local NEX_HideSurvivorIconText = "NxH"

local function NEX_GetSurvivorSlots()
    local slots = {}
    local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if not playerGui then return slots end

    for _, gui in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
        if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
            for _, descendant in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("GuiObject") and (descendant.Name:match("^Survivor%d+$") or descendant:GetAttribute("UserId") ~= nil) then
                    local card = descendant
                    local imageLabel = card:FindFirstChild("ImageLabel")
                    local viewport = card:FindFirstChild("ViewportFrame")
                    local textLabel = card:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
                    
                    if not textLabel then
                        for _, c in ipairs(card:GetDescendants()) do
                            if c:IsA("TextLabel") and c.Name ~= "NEX_CustomHookCounter" and c.Name ~= "TimerLabel" then
                                textLabel = c
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if not imageLabel then
                        for _, c in ipairs(card:GetDescendants()) do
                            if c:IsA("ImageLabel") and c.Name ~= "Icon" and c.Name ~= "Progress" and c.Name ~= "1" and c.Name ~= "2" then
                                imageLabel = c
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    table.insert(slots, {
                        Card = card,
                        ImageLabel = imageLabel,
                        ViewportFrame = viewport,
                        TextLabel = textLabel,
                        UserId = card:GetAttribute("UserId"),
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return slots
end

local function NEX_ApplyHideSurvivorIcon()
    for _, slot in ipairs(NEX_GetSurvivorSlots()) do
        local card = slot.Card

        -- 1. ViewportFrame (3D Avatar Portrait)
        local viewport = slot.ViewportFrame or (card and card:FindFirstChild("ViewportFrame"))
        if viewport and viewport:IsA("ViewportFrame") then
            if NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Originals[viewport] == nil then
                NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Originals[viewport] = {
                    Visible = viewport.Visible
                }
            end
            viewport.Visible = false
        end

        -- 2. ImageLabel (2D Portrait)
        local imageLabel = slot.ImageLabel or (card and card:FindFirstChild("ImageLabel"))
        if imageLabel and imageLabel:IsA("ImageLabel") then
            if not NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Originals[imageLabel] then
                NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Originals[imageLabel] = {
                    Image = imageLabel.Image,
                    ImageColor3 = imageLabel.ImageColor3,
                    ImageTransparency = imageLabel.ImageTransparency,
                    ImageRectOffset = imageLabel.ImageRectOffset,
                    ImageRectSize = imageLabel.ImageRectSize,
                    ScaleType = imageLabel.ScaleType,
                    Visible = imageLabel.Visible,
                }
            end

            imageLabel.Image = NEX_HideSurvivorIconImage
            imageLabel.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            imageLabel.ImageTransparency = 0
            imageLabel.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            imageLabel.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(0, 0)
            imageLabel.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
            imageLabel.Visible = true
        elseif card then
            local customIcon = card:FindFirstChild("NEX_SurvivorIconReplacement")
            if not customIcon then
                customIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                customIcon.Name = "NEX_SurvivorIconReplacement"
                customIcon.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                customIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                customIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                customIcon.Image = NEX_HideSurvivorIconImage
                customIcon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
                customIcon.ZIndex = 15
                customIcon.Parent = card
            end
            customIcon.Visible = true
        end

        -- 3. TextLabel (Player Name)
        local textLabel = slot.TextLabel or (card and card:FindFirstChild("TextLabel"))
        if textLabel and textLabel:IsA("TextLabel") and textLabel.Name ~= "NEX_CustomHookCounter" then
            if not NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Originals[textLabel] then
                NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Originals[textLabel] = {
                    Text = textLabel.Text,
                    TextColor3 = textLabel.TextColor3,
                    TextTransparency = textLabel.TextTransparency,
                }
            end

            textLabel.Text = NEX_HideSurvivorIconText
            textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            textLabel.TextTransparency = 0
        end
    end
end

local function NEX_RestoreSurvivorIcons()
    for object, original in pairs(NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Originals) do
        if object and object.Parent and original then
            pcall(function()
                if original.Image ~= nil and object:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                    object.Image = original.Image
                    object.ImageColor3 = original.ImageColor3
                    object.ImageTransparency = original.ImageTransparency
                    object.ImageRectOffset = original.ImageRectOffset
                    object.ImageRectSize = original.ImageRectSize
                    object.ScaleType = original.ScaleType
                    if original.Visible ~= nil then object.Visible = original.Visible end
                end
                if original.Visible ~= nil and object:IsA("ViewportFrame") then
                    object.Visible = original.Visible
                end
                if original.Text ~= nil and object:IsA("TextLabel") then
                    object.Text = original.Text
                    object.TextColor3 = original.TextColor3
                    object.TextTransparency = original.TextTransparency
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if playerGui then
            for _, v in ipairs(playerGui:GetDescendants()) do
                if v.Name == "NEX_SurvivorIconReplacement" then
                    v:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Originals = {}
end

local function NEX_SetHideSurvivorIcon(enabled)
    VD.VIS_HideSurvivorIcon = enabled and true or false

    if VD.VIS_HideSurvivorIcon then
        NEX_ApplyHideSurvivorIcon()
        if not NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Connection then
            NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if VD.VIS_HideSurvivorIcon then
                    NEX_ApplyHideSurvivorIcon()
                end
            end)
        end
    else
        if NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Connection then
            pcall(function() NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Connection:Disconnect() end)
            NEX_HideSurvivorIconState.Connection = nil
        end
        NEX_RestoreSurvivorIcons()
    end
end

getgenv().NEX_SetHideSurvivorIcon = NEX_SetHideSurvivorIcon
getgenv().NEX_GetSurvivorSlots = NEX_GetSurvivorSlots
getgenv().NEX_HideSurvivorIconState = NEX_HideSurvivorIconState
end)();

-- =====================================================
-- SHOW HOOK COUNTER (SURVIVOR & KILLER)
-- =====================================================
(function()
local NEX_HookCounterState = {
    Connection = nil,
    CharCache = {},
}

local function NEX_IsMarkActive(mark)
    if not mark or not mark:IsA("ImageLabel") then return false end
    local color = mark.ImageColor3
    if not color then return false end
    -- Native unhooked color is (0, 0, 0).
    -- Hooked color is (1, 1, 1) or yellow (1, 0.86, 0).
    -- Check if it's noticeably brighter than black.
    return (color.R > 0.2 or color.G > 0.2 or color.B > 0.2)
end

local function NEX_FindSurvivorCharacter(slot, card)
    local rawUid = slot.UserId or (card and card:GetAttribute("UserId"))
    local numUid = tonumber(rawUid)

    -- Cached character check
    local cached = NEX_HookCounterState.CharCache[card]
    if cached and cached.Parent and cached:IsA("Model") then
        return cached
    end

    -- 1. Try via Players service if real player
    if numUid and numUid > 0 then
        local p = game.Players:GetPlayerByUserId(numUid)
        if p and p.Character then
            NEX_HookCounterState.CharCache[card] = p.Character
            return p.Character
        end
    end

    -- 2. Try via Name (accounting for Hide Survivor Icon)
    local hideState = getgenv().NEX_HideSurvivorIconState
    local orig = hideState and hideState.Originals and hideState.Originals[slot.TextLabel]
    local nameText = (orig and orig.Text) or (slot.TextLabel and slot.TextLabel.Text) or ""
    if nameText ~= "" and nameText ~= "NxH" then
        local cleanName = nameText:gsub("%s*%(bot%)%s*$", ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if cleanName ~= "" then
            local p = game.Players:FindFirstChild(cleanName)
            if p and p.Character then
                NEX_HookCounterState.CharCache[card] = p.Character
                return p.Character
            end
            local charInWs = workspace:FindFirstChild(cleanName)
            if charInWs and charInWs:IsA("Model") then
                NEX_HookCounterState.CharCache[card] = charInWs
                return charInWs
            end
        end
    end

    -- 3. Try Bot / Stand-in in Workspace or CollectionService
    if numUid then
        local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
        local bots = CollectionService:GetTagged("SurvivorBot")
        for _, botChar in ipairs(bots) do
            if botChar:IsA("Model") then
                local botId = tonumber(botChar:GetAttribute("BotId"))
                local replaces = tonumber(botChar:GetAttribute("ReplacesUserId"))
                if (botId and botId == numUid) or (replaces and replaces == numUid) then
                    NEX_HookCounterState.CharCache[card] = botChar
                    return botChar
                end
            end
        end

        for _, model in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if model:IsA("Model") then
                local botId = tonumber(model:GetAttribute("BotId"))
                local replaces = tonumber(model:GetAttribute("ReplacesUserId"))
                local mUid = tonumber(model:GetAttribute("UserId"))
                if (botId and botId == numUid) or (replaces and replaces == numUid) or (mUid and mUid == numUid) then
                    NEX_HookCounterState.CharCache[card] = model
                    return model
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function NEX_GetCardHookCount(card, slot)
    -- 1. Check Native Tally Marks on the card
    local tallyCount = 0
    local mark1 = card:FindFirstChild("1")
    local mark2 = card:FindFirstChild("2")
    if NEX_IsMarkActive(mark2) then
        tallyCount = 2
    elseif NEX_IsMarkActive(mark1) then
        tallyCount = 1
    end

    local killedMark = card:FindFirstChild("killed")
    local isKilled = killedMark and killedMark:IsA("ImageLabel") and killedMark.Visible and (killedMark.ImageTransparency < 0.9)
    if isKilled then
        tallyCount = 3
    end

    local hookedMark = card:FindFirstChild("hooked")
    local isHooked = hookedMark and hookedMark:IsA("ImageLabel") and hookedMark.Visible and (hookedMark.ImageTransparency < 0.9)
    if isHooked and tallyCount < 1 then
        tallyCount = 1
    end

    -- 2. Check Character model in Workspace
    local char = NEX_FindSurvivorCharacter(slot, card)
    local charHook = 0
    if char then
        charHook = tonumber(char:GetAttribute("HookCount")) or 0
        if char:GetAttribute("IsDead") == true or (char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and char.Humanoid.Health <= 0) then
            charHook = 3
        elseif char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") == true and charHook < 1 then
            charHook = 1
        end
    end

    -- 3. Check Player / Card attributes
    local playerHook = 0
    local rawUid = slot.UserId or (card and card:GetAttribute("UserId"))
    local numUid = tonumber(rawUid)
    if numUid and numUid > 0 then
        local p = game.Players:GetPlayerByUserId(numUid)
        if p then
            playerHook = tonumber(p:GetAttribute("HookCount")) or 0
            if p:GetAttribute("IsDead") == true then
                playerHook = 3
            end
        end
    end

    local cardHook = tonumber(card:GetAttribute("HookCount")) or 0

    -- 4. Multi-source resolution: take highest valid count
    local finalHook = math.max(tallyCount, charHook, playerHook, cardHook)
    return math.clamp(finalHook, 0, 3)
end

local function NEX_UpdateHookCounter(enabled)
    local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if not playerGui then return end

    local getSlots = getgenv().NEX_GetSurvivorSlots
    local slots = getSlots and getSlots() or {}

    for _, slot in ipairs(slots) do
        local card = slot.Card
        if card then
            local labelName = "NEX_CustomHookCounter"
            local customLabel = card:FindFirstChild(labelName)

            if enabled then
                local hookCount = NEX_GetCardHookCount(card, slot)

                if not customLabel then
                    customLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    customLabel.Name = labelName
                    customLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.3, 0)
                    customLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.7, 0)
                    customLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    customLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
                    customLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    customLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    customLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                    customLabel.TextScaled = true
                    customLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                    customLabel.ZIndex = 25
                    Instance.new("UICorner", customLabel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
                    customLabel.Parent = card
                end

                customLabel.Visible = true
                if hookCount >= 3 then
                    customLabel.Text = "DEAD (3/3)"
                    customLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 75, 75)
                else
                    customLabel.Text = "Hooks: " .. tostring(hookCount) .. "/3"
                    if hookCount == 2 then
                        customLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 0)
                    elseif hookCount == 1 then
                        customLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
                    else
                        customLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    end
                end
            else
                if customLabel then
                    customLabel.Visible = false
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function NEX_SetShowHookCounter(enabled)
    VD.VIS_ShowHookCounter = enabled and true or false

    if VD.VIS_ShowHookCounter then
        NEX_UpdateHookCounter(true)
        if not NEX_HookCounterState.Connection then
            NEX_HookCounterState.Connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if VD.VIS_ShowHookCounter then
                    NEX_UpdateHookCounter(true)
                end
            end)
        end
    else
        if NEX_HookCounterState.Connection then
            pcall(function() NEX_HookCounterState.Connection:Disconnect() end)
            NEX_HookCounterState.Connection = nil
        end
        NEX_HookCounterState.CharCache = {}
        NEX_UpdateHookCounter(false)
        pcall(function()
            local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if playerGui then
                for _, v in ipairs(playerGui:GetDescendants()) do
                    if v.Name == "NEX_CustomHookCounter" then
                        v:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

getgenv().NEX_SetShowHookCounter = NEX_SetShowHookCounter
end)();


-- =====================================================
-- SHOW PING & FPS
-- =====================================================
(function()
local NEX_PingFPSState = {
    Gui = nil,
    Connection = nil,
    Frames = 0,
    LastUpdate = 0,
}

local function NEX_GetPingValue()
    local ok, value = pcall(function()
        local stats = game:GetService("Stats")
        local network = stats and stats:FindFirstChild("Network")
        local serverStats = network and network:FindFirstChild("ServerStatsItem")
        local dataPing = serverStats and serverStats:FindFirstChild("Data Ping")
        if dataPing and dataPing.GetValue then
            return math.floor(dataPing:GetValue() + 0.5)
        end
        if dataPing and dataPing.GetValueString then
            local raw = tostring(dataPing:GetValueString())
            return tonumber(raw:match("%d+"))
        end
    end)
    if ok and value then return value end
    return nil
end

local function NEX_CreatePingFPSGui()
    local parent = GetSafeGuiParent()
    if not parent then return nil end

    local old = parent:FindFirstChild("NEX_PingFPSGui")
    if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end

    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name = "NEX_PingFPSGui"
    sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
    sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    sg.Parent = parent

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = "Main"
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 118, 0, 44)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 120)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 18, 24)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Parent = sg
    Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(96, 72, 160)
    stroke.Thickness = 1

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = "PingFPSLabel"
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -8)
    label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 4)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextSize = 13
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 235, 245)
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    label.Text = "PING: --ms\nFPS: --"
    label.Parent = frame

    return sg
end

local function NEX_SetShowPingFPS(enabled)
    VD.VIS_ShowPingFPS = enabled and true or false

    if VD.VIS_ShowPingFPS then
        NEX_PingFPSState.Gui = NEX_PingFPSState.Gui or NEX_CreatePingFPSGui()
        NEX_PingFPSState.Frames = 0
        NEX_PingFPSState.LastUpdate = tick()

        if not NEX_PingFPSState.Connection then
            NEX_PingFPSState.Connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if not VD.VIS_ShowPingFPS then return end

                NEX_PingFPSState.Frames = NEX_PingFPSState.Frames + 1
                local now = tick()
                if now - NEX_PingFPSState.LastUpdate < 0.5 then return end

                local fps = math.floor(NEX_PingFPSState.Frames / (now - NEX_PingFPSState.LastUpdate) + 0.5)
                local ping = NEX_GetPingValue()
                NEX_PingFPSState.Frames = 0
                NEX_PingFPSState.LastUpdate = now

                if not (NEX_PingFPSState.Gui and NEX_PingFPSState.Gui.Parent) then
                    NEX_PingFPSState.Gui = NEX_CreatePingFPSGui()
                end

                local label = NEX_PingFPSState.Gui and NEX_PingFPSState.Gui:FindFirstChild("PingFPSLabel", true)
                if label then
                    label.Text = ("PING: %sms\nFPS: %d"):format(ping and tostring(ping) or "--", fps)
                end
            end)
        end
    else
        if NEX_PingFPSState.Connection then
            pcall(function() NEX_PingFPSState.Connection:Disconnect() end)
            NEX_PingFPSState.Connection = nil
        end
        if NEX_PingFPSState.Gui then
            pcall(function() NEX_PingFPSState.Gui:Destroy() end)
            NEX_PingFPSState.Gui = nil
        end
    end
end

getgenv().NEX_SetShowPingFPS = NEX_SetShowPingFPS
end)();

-- =====================================================
-- SILENT AIM: FLASHLIGHT
-- =====================================================
(function()
local NEX_FlashlightAimState = {
    Connection = nil,
    LaserBeam = nil,
    FlashlightPart = nil,
    Active = false,
}

local function NEX_GetFlashlightActivateRemote()
    local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local items = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Items")
    local flashlight = items and items:FindFirstChild("Flashlight")
    local activate = flashlight and flashlight:FindFirstChild("Activate")
    if activate and activate:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        return activate
    end
    return nil
end

local function NEX_GetFlashlightTargetPart(char)
    if not char then return nil end
    local preferred = VD.FLASH_TargetPart or "Head"
    local part = char:FindFirstChild(preferred)
    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return part end
    return char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function NEX_IsAliveCharacter(char)
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    local state = char:GetAttribute("State")
    return state ~= "Dead"
end

local function NEX_GetFlashlightTarget()
    local localChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    local localRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not localRoot then return nil end

    local maxRange = tonumber(VD.FLASH_Range) or 120
    local bestPart, bestScore = nil, math.huge

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character and NEX_IsAliveCharacter(player.Character) then
            local isKiller = player.Team and player.Team.Name == "Killer"
            if isKiller then
                local part = NEX_GetFlashlightTargetPart(player.Character)
                if part then
                    local dist = (localRoot.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist <= maxRange and dist < bestScore then
                        bestScore = dist
                        bestPart = part
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return bestPart
end

local function NEX_ClearFlashlightLaser()
    if NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam then
        pcall(function() NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam:Destroy() end)
        NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam = nil
    end
end

local function NEX_GetFlashlightOrigin(cam)
    local source = NEX_FlashlightAimState.FlashlightPart
    if typeof and typeof(source) == "Instance" then
        if source:IsA("BasePart") then
            return source.Position
        end
        local part = source:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        if part then
            return part.Position
        end
    end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hand = char and (
        char:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
        or char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
        or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    )
    if hand and hand:IsA("BasePart") then
        return hand.Position
    end

    return cam and cam.CFrame.Position or nil
end

local function NEX_UpdateFlashlightLaser(originPos, targetPos)
    if not NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam then
        local laser = Instance.new("Part")
        laser.Name = "FlashlightSilentAimLaser"
        laser.Anchored = true
        laser.CanCollide = false
        laser.CanTouch = false
        laser.CastShadow = false
        laser.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        laser.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 255)
        laser.Transparency = 0
        laser.Parent = workspace
        NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam = laser
    end

    local dist = (targetPos - originPos).Magnitude
    if dist < 0.1 then return end

    local laser = NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam
    laser.Size = Vector3.new(0.16, 0.16, dist)
    laser.CFrame = CFrame.new((originPos + targetPos) / 2, targetPos)
    laser.Transparency = 0
end

local function NEX_FlashlightAimStep()
    if getgenv().NexHubTier ~= "Premium" then
        if NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam then
            NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam.Transparency = 1
        end
        return
    end

    if not (VD.FLASH_SilentAim and NEX_FlashlightAimState.Active) then
        if NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam then
            NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam.Transparency = 1
        end
        return
    end

    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local targetPart = NEX_GetFlashlightTarget()
    if not (cam and targetPart) then
        if NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam then
            NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam.Transparency = 1
        end
        return
    end

    local targetPos = targetPart.Position
    local smooth = math.clamp(tonumber(VD.FLASH_Smooth) or 0.35, 0.05, 1)
    local originPos = NEX_GetFlashlightOrigin(cam)

    if VD.FLASH_Laser and originPos then
        NEX_UpdateFlashlightLaser(originPos, targetPos)
    elseif NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam then
        NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam.Transparency = 1
    end

    pcall(function()
        cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, targetPos), smooth)
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(targetPos.X, hrp.Position.Y, targetPos.Z))
        end
    end)
end

local function NEX_StartFlashlightSilentAim()
    getgenv().NEX_FlashlightActivateRemote = NEX_GetFlashlightActivateRemote()
    if NEX_FlashlightAimState.Connection then return end
    NEX_FlashlightAimState.Connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(NEX_FlashlightAimStep)
end

local function NEX_StopFlashlightSilentAim()
    NEX_FlashlightAimState.Active = false
    NEX_FlashlightAimState.FlashlightPart = nil
    NEX_ClearFlashlightLaser()
    if NEX_FlashlightAimState.Connection then
        pcall(function() NEX_FlashlightAimState.Connection:Disconnect() end)
        NEX_FlashlightAimState.Connection = nil
    end
end

local function NEX_SetFlashlightSilentAim(enabled)
    if enabled and getgenv().NexHubTier ~= "Premium" then
        VD.FLASH_SilentAim = false
        NEX_StopFlashlightSilentAim()
        return
    end

    VD.FLASH_SilentAim = enabled and true or false
    if VD.FLASH_SilentAim then
        NEX_StartFlashlightSilentAim()
    else
        NEX_StopFlashlightSilentAim()
    end
end

getgenv().NEX_SetFlashlightSilentAim = NEX_SetFlashlightSilentAim
getgenv().NEX_ClearFlashlightLaser = NEX_ClearFlashlightLaser
getgenv().NEX_SetFlashlightAimActive = function(active, flashlightPart)
    NEX_FlashlightAimState.Active = active and true or false
    if NEX_FlashlightAimState.Active and flashlightPart then
        NEX_FlashlightAimState.FlashlightPart = flashlightPart
    elseif not NEX_FlashlightAimState.Active then
        NEX_FlashlightAimState.FlashlightPart = nil
    end
    if not NEX_FlashlightAimState.Active and NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam then
        NEX_FlashlightAimState.LaserBeam.Transparency = 1
    end
end
getgenv().NEX_FlashlightActivateRemote = NEX_GetFlashlightActivateRemote()
end)();
