        _lastRemoteSend = 0
    end

    local function _applyInvis(char)
        if not char then return false end
        _saveState(char)
        _setBodyAlpha(char, true)
        _connectDescHandler(char)
        _createIndicator(char)
        _startRemote()
        return true
    end

    local function _removeInvis(char)
        _stopRemote()
        _disconnectDescHandler()
        _removeIndicators()
        if char then _setBodyAlpha(char, false) end
        _restoreState()
    end

    local function _updateFloatingGui()
        if not Invisibility.toggleButton then return end
        if Invisibility.active then
            Invisibility.toggleButton.Text = "ON"
            Invisibility.toggleButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 120)
            Invisibility.toggleButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 70, 40)
        else
            Invisibility.toggleButton.Text = "OFF"
            Invisibility.toggleButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
            Invisibility.toggleButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55)
        end
        if Invisibility._resizeFloating then pcall(Invisibility._resizeFloating) end
    end

    local function _syncMainToggle()
        if not Invisibility.mainToggle or Invisibility.cleaned then return end
        Invisibility.syncingMainToggle = true
        pcall(function() Invisibility.mainToggle:SetValue(Invisibility.active) end)
        Invisibility.syncingMainToggle = false
    end

    local function setInvisState(enabled, doNotify)
        if Invisibility.cleaned then return false end
        enabled = enabled == true

        if enabled == Invisibility.active then
            VD.InvisibleNotVisual = enabled
            _updateFloatingGui(); _syncMainToggle()
            return true
        end

        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if enabled then
            if not char then
                VD.InvisibleNotVisual = false; Invisibility.active = false
                _updateFloatingGui(); _syncMainToggle()
                if doNotify then VD_Notify("Invisible", "Character belum siap", 2) end
                return false
            end
            if not _applyInvis(char) then
                Invisibility.active = false; VD.InvisibleNotVisual = false
                _updateFloatingGui(); _syncMainToggle()
                return false
            end
            Invisibility.active = true; VD.InvisibleNotVisual = true
        else
            Invisibility.active = false; VD.InvisibleNotVisual = false
            _removeInvis(char)
        end

        _updateFloatingGui(); _syncMainToggle()
        if doNotify then VD_Notify("Invisible", enabled and "Enabled" or "Disabled", 2) end
        return true
    end

    local function _createFloatingGui()
        if Invisibility.screenGui and Invisibility.screenGui.Parent then
            Invisibility.screenGui.Enabled = true
            _updateFloatingGui()
            return Invisibility.screenGui
        end

        local okCore, coreGui = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        local guiParent = (okCore and coreGui) and coreGui or LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not guiParent then return nil end

        local old = guiParent:FindFirstChild("NexHub_InvisGUI")
        if old then old:Destroy() end

        local screen = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        screen.Name = "NexHub_InvisGUI"
        screen.ResetOnSpawn = false
        screen.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        screen.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        screen.DisplayOrder = 100
        screen.Parent = guiParent

        local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
        frame.Name = "DragFrame"
        frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(118, 56)
        frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -59, 0.78, 0)
        frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
        frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        frame.Active = true
        frame.ClipsDescendants = true
        frame.Parent = screen
        Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55); stroke.Thickness = 1; stroke.Transparency = 0.35

        local titleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
        titleLbl.Name = "Title"; titleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        titleLbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4); titleLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -44, 0, 20)
        titleLbl.Text = "Invisible"; titleLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
        titleLbl.TextSize = 13; titleLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        titleLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

        local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", frame)
        closeBtn.Name = "CloseBtn"; closeBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18)
        closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 5)
        closeBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1; closeBtn.Text = "X"
        closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 170); closeBtn.TextSize = 13
        closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold

        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", frame)
        btn.Name = "ToggleButton"; btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 24)
        btn.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 26)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55); btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0; btn.Text = "OFF"
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230); btn.TextSize = 13
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; btn.AutoButtonColor = true
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

        local function resizeFrame()
            if not frame or not frame.Parent then return end
            local ts = game:GetService("TextService")
            local tw = ts:GetTextSize(titleLbl.Text, titleLbl.TextSize, titleLbl.Font, Vector2.new(400,50)).X
            local bw = ts:GetTextSize(btn.Text or "OFF", btn.TextSize, btn.Font, Vector2.new(400,50)).X
            local w = math.clamp(math.max(tw + 58, bw + 28, 96), 96, 160)
            frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.ceil(w), 56)
        end
        Invisibility._resizeFloating = resizeFrame
        resizeFrame()

        closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() screen.Enabled = false end)

        local drag = { dragging=false, moved=false, dragStart=nil, startPos=nil, dragInput=nil, threshold=8 }

        local function beginDrag(inp)
            if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
            drag.dragging = true; drag.moved = false
            drag.dragStart = inp.Position; drag.startPos = frame.Position
        end
        local function updateDrag(inp)
            if not drag.dragging then return end
            local it = inp.UserInputType
            if it ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch and it ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then return end
            local delta = inp.Position - drag.dragStart
            if not drag.moved and delta.Magnitude >= drag.threshold then drag.moved = true end
            if drag.moved then
                frame.Position = UDim2.new(drag.startPos.X.Scale, drag.startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, drag.startPos.Y.Scale, drag.startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
            end
        end
        local function endDrag(inp)
            if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
            drag.dragging = false; drag.dragInput = nil
        end

        frame.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag); titleLbl.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag); btn.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag)
        frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then drag.dragInput = i end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i) if drag.dragging and i == drag.dragInput then updateDrag(i) end end)
        UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(endDrag)

        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            if drag.moved then drag.moved = false; return end
            setInvisState(not Invisibility.active, true)
        end)

        Invisibility.screenGui  = screen
        Invisibility.frame      = frame
        Invisibility.toggleButton = btn
        Invisibility.closeButton  = closeBtn
        _updateFloatingGui()
        return screen
    end

    function Invisibility.Cleanup()
        if Invisibility.cleaned then return end
        Invisibility.cleaned = true; Invisibility.active = false; VD.InvisibleNotVisual = false
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        _stopRemote(); _disconnectDescHandler(); _removeIndicators()
        if char then _setBodyAlpha(char, false) end
        _restoreState()
        if Invisibility.characterAddedConnection   then Invisibility.characterAddedConnection:Disconnect();   Invisibility.characterAddedConnection   = nil end
        if Invisibility.characterRemovingConnection then Invisibility.characterRemovingConnection:Disconnect(); Invisibility.characterRemovingConnection = nil end
        if Invisibility.screenGui then Invisibility.screenGui:Destroy(); Invisibility.screenGui = nil end
        Invisibility.toggleButton = nil; Invisibility.closeButton = nil
        Invisibility.frame = nil; Invisibility._resizeFloating = nil
    end

    Invisibility.characterRemovingConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
        if Invisibility.active then
            _stopRemote(); _disconnectDescHandler(); _removeIndicators(); _restoreState()
        end
    end)

    Invisibility.characterAddedConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.spawn(function()
            local hum  = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            local root = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            if not hum or not root or Invisibility.cleaned then return end
            if Invisibility.active then
                _clearStored(); _applyInvis(char); _updateFloatingGui()
            end
        end)
    end)

    getgenv().__NexHub_InvisibilityController = Invisibility
    VD.InvisibleNotVisual = false

    -- Public API
    Invisibility.SetState    = setInvisState
    Invisibility.AttachMainToggle = function(tog)
        Invisibility.mainToggle = tog
        if tog then pcall(function() tog:SetValue(Invisibility.active) end) end
    end
    Invisibility.OpenGui = function()
        local gui = _createFloatingGui()
        if gui then gui.Enabled = true; _updateFloatingGui() end
    end
    Invisibility.MainToggleCallback = function(v)
        if Invisibility.syncingMainToggle then return end
        setInvisState(v, true)
    end
end

-- Compat shims untuk kode lama yang masih memanggil VD_SetInvisibleNotVisual / VD_UpdateInvisibleNotVisual
function VD_SetInvisibleNotVisual(state)
    local ctrl = getgenv().__NexHub_InvisibilityController
    if ctrl then ctrl.SetState(state == true, false) end
end
function VD_UpdateInvisibleNotVisual()
    local ctrl = getgenv().__NexHub_InvisibilityController
    if ctrl then ctrl.SetState(VD.InvisibleNotVisual == true, false) end
end

function VD_SetCharacterTransparency(character, transparency)
    for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if (descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Decal")) and descendant.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
            pcall(function() descendant.Transparency = transparency end)
        end
    end
end

local VD_LungeState = {
    OriginalBoost = nil,
    IsLunging = false,
    LungeStartTime = 0,
}

function VD_UpdateInfiniteLunge()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end

    if type(GetRole) == "function" and GetRole() ~= "Killer" then
        if VD_LungeState.OriginalBoost then
            char:SetAttribute("lungeboost", VD_LungeState.OriginalBoost)
            VD_LungeState.OriginalBoost = nil
        end
        VD_LungeState.IsLunging = false
        VD_LungeState.LungeStartTime = 0
        return
    end

    if VD.KILLER_InfLunge then
        -- Pasang lungeboost 999999 agar KillerActionController mengizinkan hold lunge tanpa auto-release paksa
        if char:GetAttribute("lungeboost") ~= 999999 then
            VD_LungeState.OriginalBoost = char:GetAttribute("lungeboost") or 1
            char:SetAttribute("lungeboost", 999999)
        end

        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end

        local ci = char:FindFirstChild("CheckInterractable")
        local isAction = ci and ci:GetAttribute("action") == true

        -- Deteksi apakah sedang dalam status lunge (WalkSpeed >= 25 saat action aktif)
        if isAction and hum.WalkSpeed >= 25 then
            if not VD_LungeState.IsLunging then
                VD_LungeState.IsLunging = true
                VD_LungeState.LungeStartTime = tick()
            end
        elseif (not isAction) or (hum.WalkSpeed < 18 and (tick() - VD_LungeState.LungeStartTime > 0.6)) then
            VD_LungeState.IsLunging = false
            VD_LungeState.LungeStartTime = 0
        end

        -- Adaptive Lunge Control: Cegah rollback anti-cheat server saat hold lunge berkepanjangan
        if VD_LungeState.IsLunging and isAction then
            local elapsed = tick() - VD_LungeState.LungeStartTime

            -- 0.0s - 0.52s: Burst resmi server (27 stud/s), 100% legal dan bebas rollback
            if elapsed <= 0.52 then
                if hum.WalkSpeed < 27 then
                    hum.WalkSpeed = 27
                end
            else
                -- > 0.52s: Window resmi server berakhir.
                -- Turunkan kecepatan secara halus ke kecepatan legal killer (~19.125 stud/s)
                -- sehingga server anti-cheat tidak mendeteksi speedhack / tidak memicu rollback,
                -- sementara animasi lunge dan kesiapan tebas tetap tertahan tak terbatas!
                local baseSpeed = char:GetAttribute("Speed") or 19.125
                local speedMult = char:GetAttribute("speedboost") or 1
                local safeSpeed = math.max(baseSpeed * speedMult, 19.125)

                hum.WalkSpeed = safeSpeed
            end
        end
    else
        -- Reset bersih saat fitur dinonaktifkan
        if VD_LungeState.OriginalBoost then
            char:SetAttribute("lungeboost", VD_LungeState.OriginalBoost)
            VD_LungeState.OriginalBoost = nil
        end
        VD_LungeState.IsLunging = false
        VD_LungeState.LungeStartTime = 0
    end
end


local VD_MoonwalkState = {
    LastEnabled = false,
    Yaw = nil,
    Sway = 0,
    ButtonGui = nil,
    Button = nil,
    ButtonLabel = nil,
    SyncingUI = false,
}

function VD_RefreshMoonwalkButton()
    local btn = VD_MoonwalkState.Button
    if not (btn and btn.Parent) then return end
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = VD.Moonwalk and Color3.fromRGB(35, 185, 95) or Color3.fromRGB(20, 0, 30)
    local label = VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonLabel
    if label and label.Parent then
        label.Text = VD.Moonwalk and "ON" or "OFF"
        label.TextColor3 = VD.Moonwalk and Color3.fromRGB(190, 255, 210) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    end
end

function VD_SetMoonwalk(state)
    VD.Moonwalk = state and true or false
    VD_RefreshMoonwalkButton()
end
getgenv().VD_SetMoonwalk = VD_SetMoonwalk

function VD_DestroyMoonwalkButton()
    if VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonGui then
        pcall(function() VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonGui:Destroy() end)
    end
    VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonGui = nil
    VD_MoonwalkState.Button = nil
    VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonLabel = nil
end

function VD_CreateMoonwalkButton()
    local parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
    if not parent then
        task.delay(1, VD_CreateMoonwalkButton)
        return
    end
    if VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonGui and VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonGui.Parent then
        VD_RefreshMoonwalkButton()
        return
    end

    local old = parent:FindFirstChild("NEX_MoonwalkButton")
    if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end

    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name = "NEX_MoonwalkButton"
    sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
    sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    sg.DisplayOrder = 999999
    sg.Parent = parent

    local btn = Instance.new("ImageButton")
    btn.Name = "MoonwalkButton"
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 60)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0.88, 0, 0.43, 0)
    btn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 0, 30)
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.AutoButtonColor = true
    btn.Visible = true
    btn.ZIndex = 10
    btn.Parent = sg
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.Transparency = 0.2

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
    lbl.Name = "StateLabel"
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = "OFF"
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    lbl.TextScaled = true
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    lbl.ZIndex = 11

    local function applyShine(obj, baseColor)
        local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient", obj)
        grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, baseColor),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, baseColor),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, baseColor),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, baseColor)
        })
        grad.Rotation = 45
        grad.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, -1)

        task.spawn(function()
            local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
            local ti = TweenInfo.new(2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1)
            local tw = TweenService:Create(grad, ti, { Offset = Vector2.new(1, 1) })
            tw:Play()
        end)
    end

    pcall(applyShine, btn, Color3.fromRGB(20, 0, 30))
    pcall(applyShine, lbl, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255))
    pcall(applyShine, stroke, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255))

    local dragging = false
    local dragStart, startPos
    local moved = false
    btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            moved = false
            if VD.MoonwalkButtonLocked then return end
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = btn.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if VD.MoonwalkButtonLocked then return end
        if not dragging or not dragStart or not startPos then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        if math.abs(delta.X) > 4 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 4 then moved = true end
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end)

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if moved and not VD.MoonwalkButtonLocked then return end
        moved = false
        VD_SetMoonwalk(not VD.Moonwalk)
    end)

    VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonGui = sg
    VD_MoonwalkState.Button = btn
    VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonLabel = lbl
    VD_RefreshMoonwalkButton()
end

function VD_SetMoonwalkButtonVisible(state)
    VD.MoonwalkButton = state and true or false
    local flagName = VD_To_Flag and VD_To_Flag.MoonwalkButton
    local elem = flagName and Window and Window.ConfigElements and Window.ConfigElements[flagName]
    if elem and elem.Set and not VD_MoonwalkState.SyncingUI then
        VD_MoonwalkState.SyncingUI = true
        pcall(function() elem:Set(VD.MoonwalkButton) end)
        VD_MoonwalkState.SyncingUI = false
    end

    if VD.MoonwalkButton then
        VD_CreateMoonwalkButton()
    else
        VD_SetMoonwalk(false)
        VD_DestroyMoonwalkButton()
    end
end
getgenv().VD_SetMoonwalkButtonVisible = VD_SetMoonwalkButtonVisible

task.spawn(function()
    while getgenv().VD and not getgenv().VD.Destroyed do
        if VD.MoonwalkButton and not (VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonGui and VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonGui.Parent) then
            pcall(VD_CreateMoonwalkButton)
        elseif VD.MoonwalkButton then
            VD_RefreshMoonwalkButton()
        elseif VD_MoonwalkState.ButtonGui then
            VD_DestroyMoonwalkButton()
        end
        task.wait(3)
    end
end)

-- =====================================================
-- AIM LOCK - External Toggle Button + Logic
-- =====================================================
do
local VD_AimLockState = {
    Active = false,
    CurrentTarget = nil,
    ButtonGui = nil,
    Button = nil,
    ButtonLabel = nil,
    SyncingUI = false,
}

local function VD_AimLock_IsSurvivor(p)
    return p.Team and p.Team.Name == "Survivors"
end

local function VD_AimLock_IsDowned(character)
    if not character then return true end
    if character:GetAttribute("Knocked") == true then return true end
    if character:GetAttribute("IsHooked") == true then return true end
    local hum = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if hum and hum.Health <= 0 then return true end
    return false
end

local function VD_AimLock_GetClosest()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return nil end

    local maxDist = VD.AimLockMaxDistance or 50
    local bestTarget = nil
    local bestDistance = maxDist + 1

    for _, otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if otherPlayer ~= LocalPlayer and otherPlayer.Character and VD_AimLock_IsSurvivor(otherPlayer) then
            if not VD_AimLock_IsDowned(otherPlayer.Character) then
                local otherHrp = otherPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local otherHum = otherPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

                if otherHrp and otherHum and otherHum.Health > 0 then
                    local distance = (otherHrp.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                    if distance <= maxDist and distance < bestDistance then
                        bestDistance = distance
                        bestTarget = otherHrp
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return bestTarget
end

local function VD_RefreshAimLockButton()
    local btn = VD_AimLockState.Button
    if not (btn and btn.Parent) then return end
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = VD_AimLockState.Active and Color3.fromRGB(185, 50, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(20, 0, 30)
    local label = VD_AimLockState.ButtonLabel
    if label and label.Parent then
        label.Text = VD_AimLockState.Active and "ON" or "OFF"
        label.TextColor3 = VD_AimLockState.Active and Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 200) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    end
end

local function VD_SetAimLockActive(state)
    VD_AimLockState.Active = state and true or false
    if not VD_AimLockState.Active then
        VD_AimLockState.CurrentTarget = nil
    end
    VD_RefreshAimLockButton()
end
getgenv().VD_SetAimLockActive = VD_SetAimLockActive

local function VD_DestroyAimLockButton()
    if VD_AimLockState.ButtonGui then
        pcall(function() VD_AimLockState.ButtonGui:Destroy() end)
    end
    VD_AimLockState.ButtonGui = nil
    VD_AimLockState.Button = nil
    VD_AimLockState.ButtonLabel = nil
end

local function VD_CreateAimLockButton()
    local parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
    if not parent then
        task.delay(1, VD_CreateAimLockButton)
        return
    end
    if VD_AimLockState.ButtonGui and VD_AimLockState.ButtonGui.Parent then
        VD_RefreshAimLockButton()
        return
    end

    local old = parent:FindFirstChild("NEX_AimLockButton")
    if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end

    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name = "NEX_AimLockButton"
    sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
    sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    sg.DisplayOrder = 999998
    sg.Parent = parent

    local btn = Instance.new("ImageButton")
    btn.Name = "AimLockButton"
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 60)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0.88, 0, 0.55, 0)
    btn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 0, 30)
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.AutoButtonColor = true
    btn.Visible = true
    btn.ZIndex = 10
    btn.Parent = sg
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.Transparency = 0.2

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
    lbl.Name = "StateLabel"
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = "OFF"
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    lbl.TextScaled = true
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    lbl.ZIndex = 11

    local function applyShine(obj, baseColor)
        local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient", obj)
        grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, baseColor),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, baseColor),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, baseColor),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, baseColor)
        })
        grad.Rotation = 45
        grad.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, -1)

        task.spawn(function()
            local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
            local ti = TweenInfo.new(2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1)
            local tw = TweenService:Create(grad, ti, { Offset = Vector2.new(1, 1) })
            tw:Play()
        end)
    end

    pcall(applyShine, btn, Color3.fromRGB(20, 0, 30))
    pcall(applyShine, lbl, Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50))
    pcall(applyShine, stroke, Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50))

    local dragging = false
    local dragStart, startPos
    local moved = false
    btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            moved = false
            if VD.AimLockButtonLocked then return end
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = btn.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if VD.AimLockButtonLocked then return end
        if not dragging or not dragStart or not startPos then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        if math.abs(delta.X) > 4 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 4 then moved = true end
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end)

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if moved and not VD.AimLockButtonLocked then return end
        moved = false
        VD_SetAimLockActive(not VD_AimLockState.Active)
    end)

    VD_AimLockState.ButtonGui = sg
    VD_AimLockState.Button = btn
    VD_AimLockState.ButtonLabel = lbl
    VD_RefreshAimLockButton()
end

local function VD_SetAimLockButtonVisible(state)
    VD.AimLockButton = state and true or false
    local flagName = VD_To_Flag and VD_To_Flag.AimLockButton
    local elem = flagName and Window and Window.ConfigElements and Window.ConfigElements[flagName]
    if elem and elem.Set and not VD_AimLockState.SyncingUI then
        VD_AimLockState.SyncingUI = true
        pcall(function() elem:Set(VD.AimLockButton) end)
        VD_AimLockState.SyncingUI = false
    end

    if VD.AimLockButton then
        VD_CreateAimLockButton()
    else
        VD_SetAimLockActive(false)
        VD_DestroyAimLockButton()
    end
end
getgenv().VD_SetAimLockButtonVisible = VD_SetAimLockButtonVisible

-- Keybind B untuk toggle Aim Lock
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.B and VD.AimLockButton then
        VD_SetAimLockActive(not VD_AimLockState.Active)
    end
end)

-- Reset aim lock saat respawn
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if VD_AimLockState.Active then
        VD_SetAimLockActive(false)
    end
    VD_AimLockState.CurrentTarget = nil
end)

-- Keep-alive loop untuk Aim Lock button
task.spawn(function()
    while getgenv().VD and not getgenv().VD.Destroyed do
        if VD.AimLockButton and not (VD_AimLockState.ButtonGui and VD_AimLockState.ButtonGui.Parent) then
            pcall(VD_CreateAimLockButton)
        elseif VD.AimLockButton then
            VD_RefreshAimLockButton()
        elseif VD_AimLockState.ButtonGui then
            VD_DestroyAimLockButton()
        end
        task.wait(3)
    end
end)

-- Aim Lock update setiap frame (RenderStepped)
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not VD_AimLockState.Active or not VD.AimLockButton then
        VD_AimLockState.CurrentTarget = nil
        return
    end

    local targetPart = VD_AimLock_GetClosest()
    if not targetPart then
        VD_AimLockState.CurrentTarget = nil
        return
    end

    VD_AimLockState.CurrentTarget = targetPart
    pcall(function()
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, targetPart.Position)
    end)
end)
end -- end AimLock scope

function VD_UpdateMoonwalk(deltaTime)
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera

    if VD.Moonwalk ~= VD_MoonwalkState.LastEnabled then
        if hum then hum.AutoRotate = not VD.Moonwalk end
        VD_MoonwalkState.LastEnabled = VD.Moonwalk
        if VD.Moonwalk and root then
            local _, y = root.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
            VD_MoonwalkState.Yaw = math.deg(y)
        end
    end

    if not VD.Moonwalk then
        return
    end
    if not root or not hum or not cam or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

    hum.AutoRotate = false
    local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
    local targetYaw = math.deg(math.atan2(look.X, look.Z)) + 180
    local currentYaw = VD_MoonwalkState.Yaw or targetYaw
    local diff = (targetYaw - currentYaw + 180) % 360 - 180
    local lerpSpeed = 0.22 * math.clamp((deltaTime or 1 / 60) * 60, 0, 3)
    currentYaw = currentYaw + diff * lerpSpeed
    VD_MoonwalkState.Yaw = currentYaw

    local moving = hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.01
    local targetSway = 0
    if moving then
        targetSway = math.sin(tick() * (VD.MoonwalkZigzagSpeed or 11)) * 48
    end
    VD_MoonwalkState.Sway = (VD_MoonwalkState.Sway or 0) + (targetSway - (VD_MoonwalkState.Sway or 0)) * 0.38
    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(currentYaw + VD_MoonwalkState.Sway), 0)

    if moving then
        hum:Move(hum.MoveDirection * (VD.MoonwalkBoostPower or 1.08), false)
    end
end

LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
    -- Invis controller punya listener sendiri; di sini hanya cleanup Moonwalk
    VD_MoonwalkState.LastEnabled = false
    VD_MoonwalkState.Yaw = nil
    VD_MoonwalkState.Sway = 0
end)

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(1)
    if VD.InvisibleNotVisual then pcall(VD_UpdateInvisibleNotVisual) end
end)

local VD_PalletwrongConnection = nil
local VD_PalletwrongActive = false

local function VD_GetPalletTarget(inst)
    if not inst then return nil end

    -- 1. Cek Model atau Folder langsung
    if inst:IsA("Model") or inst:IsA("Folder") then
        local nameLower = inst.Name:lower()
        if nameLower:find("pallet") then
            if inst ~= Workspace and (not Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") or inst ~= Workspace.Map) then
                return inst
            end
        end
        if inst:FindFirstChild("PalletPoint") or inst:FindFirstChild("PalletPointSlide") or inst:FindFirstChild("PrimaryPartPallet") then
            return inst
        end
    end

    -- 2. Cek BasePart yang merupakan bagian dari pallet
    if inst:IsA("BasePart") then
        local nameLower = inst.Name:lower()
        if nameLower:find("pallet") or inst.Name == "PalletPoint" or inst.Name == "PalletPointSlide" or inst.Name == "PrimaryPartPallet" then
            local p = inst.Parent
            if p and (p:IsA("Model") or p:IsA("Folder")) and p ~= Workspace and (not Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") or p ~= Workspace.Map) then
                return p
            end
            return inst
        end
    end

    return nil
end

function VD_DestroyPalletwrong(inst)
    local target = VD_GetPalletTarget(inst)
    if target and target.Parent then
        pcall(function() target:Destroy() end)
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function VD_StartRemovePalletwrong()
    if VD_PalletwrongActive then return end
    VD_PalletwrongActive = true

    task.spawn(function()
        local destroyedCount = 0
        local destroyedMap = {}

        local function destroyTarget(target)
            if target and target.Parent and not destroyedMap[target] then
                destroyedMap[target] = true
                local ok = pcall(function() target:Destroy() end)
                if ok then
                    destroyedCount = destroyedCount + 1
                end
            end
        end

        -- 1. Hapus dari cache NEX_Cache.Pallets (instan)
        if NEX_Cache and NEX_Cache.Pallets then
            for _, pal in ipairs(NEX_Cache.Pallets) do
                if pal.model and pal.model.Parent then
                    destroyTarget(pal.model)
                elseif pal.part and pal.part.Parent then
                    destroyTarget(pal.part)
                end
            end
        end

        -- 2. Hapus dari CollectionService tagged
        pcall(function()
            local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
            for _, tag in ipairs({"PalletPointSlide", "PalletPoint", "Pallet", "Palletwrong"}) do
                for _, obj in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged(tag)) do
                    local target = VD_GetPalletTarget(obj) or obj
                    destroyTarget(target)
                end
            end
        end)

        -- 3. Scan semua descendants di Map untuk membersihkan seluruh pallet yang ada
        pcall(function()
            local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") or Workspace
            for _, obj in ipairs(map:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj.Parent and not destroyedMap[obj] then
                    local target = VD_GetPalletTarget(obj)
                    if target then
                        destroyTarget(target)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        if destroyedCount > 0 then
            pcall(VD_Notify, "Pallets Removed", "Berhasil menghapus " .. tostring(destroyedCount) .. " pallet di map!", 3)
        else
            pcall(VD_Notify, "Remove Pallets", "Tidak ada pallet aktif yang ditemukan di map.", 3)
        end
    end)

    if not VD_PalletwrongConnection then
        VD_PalletwrongConnection = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(inst)
            if VD.KILLER_NoPalletStun then
                local target = VD_GetPalletTarget(inst)
                if target and target.Parent then
                    pcall(function() target:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

function VD_StopRemovePalletwrong()
    VD_PalletwrongActive = false
    if VD_PalletwrongConnection then
        pcall(function() VD_PalletwrongConnection:Disconnect() end)
        VD_PalletwrongConnection = nil
    end
end

function VD_UpdateRemovePalletwrong()
    if VD.KILLER_NoPalletStun then
        VD_StartRemovePalletwrong()
    else
        VD_StopRemovePalletwrong()
    end
end

do
    local vu = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
    LocalPlayer.Idled:Connect(function()
        if not VD.AntiAFK then return end
        pcall(function()
            vu:Button2Down(Vector2.new(0, 0), Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame)
            task.wait(0.2)
            vu:Button2Up(Vector2.new(0, 0), Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame)
        end)
    end)
end

-- =====================================================
-- MAP CACHE (Generators / Gates / Hooks / Pallets / Windows)
-- =====================================================
local NEX_Cache = {
    Generators  = {},
    Gates       = {},
    Hooks       = {},
    Pallets     = {},
    Windows     = {},
    ClosestHook = nil,
    ExitPos     = nil
}

function NEX_ScanMap()
    local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    if not map then
        NEX_Cache = {
            Generators = {}, Zombies = {}, Gates = {}, Hooks = {}, Pallets = {}, Windows = {}, ClosestHook = nil, ExitPos = nil, ExitPart = nil
        }
        return
    end

    local newGens, newZombies, newGates, newHooks, newPallets, newWindows = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}
    local exitPos = nil
    local exitPart = nil

    if map:FindFirstChild("churchbell") then
        exitPart = map:FindFirstChild("churchbell")
        if exitPart:IsA("Model") then exitPart = exitPart.PrimaryPart or exitPart:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") end
        if exitPart then exitPos = exitPart.Position else exitPos = Vector3.new(760.98, -20.14, -78.48) end
    end

    local finish = map:FindFirstChild("Finishline") or map:FindFirstChild("FinishLine") or map:FindFirstChild("Fininshline")
    if finish then
        local fp = finish:IsA("BasePart") and finish or (finish:IsA("Model") and finish:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart"))
        if fp then exitPos = fp.Position; exitPart = fp end
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(map:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Model") then
            local part = obj:FindFirstChild("HitBox", true) or obj:FindFirstChild("GeneratorPoint", true) or obj.PrimaryPart or obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
            if part then
                local n = obj.Name
                if n == "Generator" then
                    table.insert(newGens, { model = obj, part = part })
                elseif n == "Gate" or n == "ExitGate" or obj:FindFirstChild("ExitLever") then
                    table.insert(newGates, { model = obj, part = part })
                elseif n == "Hook" then
                    table.insert(newHooks, { model = obj, part = part })
                elseif n == "Palletwrong" or n:lower():find("pallet") then
                    table.insert(newPallets, { model = obj, part = part })
                elseif n == "Window" then
                    table.insert(newWindows, { model = obj, part = part })
                end
            end
        elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            if not exitPos and obj.Name:lower():find("finish") then
                exitPos = obj.Position
                exitPart = obj
            end
            if not exitPos and obj:IsA("MeshPart") then
                if obj.Material == Enum.Material.Limestone then
                    exitPos = Vector3.new(-947.90, 152.12, -7579.52)
                    exitPart = obj
                elseif obj.Material == Enum.Material.Leather then
                    exitPos = Vector3.new(1546.12, 152.21, -796.72)
                    exitPart = obj
                end
            end
            -- Tambahkan VaultTrigger ke cache window jika ditemukan
            if obj.Name == "VaultTrigger" then
                table.insert(newWindows, { model = obj.Parent, part = obj })
            end
            -- Tambahkan VaultPoint di dalam VaultTrigger (untuk path workspace.Map.Vaults:GetChildren()[4]:GetChildren()[2].VaultPoint)
            if obj.Name == "VaultPoint" and obj.Parent and obj.Parent.Name == "VaultTrigger" then
                table.insert(newWindows, { model = obj.Parent, part = obj })
            end
            -- Tambahkan PalletPoint dan PalletPointSlide ke cache pallet
            -- Spy confirmed: server menerima PalletPointSlide sebagai target drop
            if obj.Name == "PalletPoint" or obj.Name == "PalletPointSlide" then
                table.insert(newPallets, { model = obj.Parent, part = obj })
            end
        end
    end

    NEX_Cache.Generators = newGens
    NEX_Cache.Gates      = newGates
    NEX_Cache.Hooks      = newHooks
    NEX_Cache.Pallets    = newPallets
    NEX_Cache.Windows    = newWindows
    NEX_Cache.ExitPos    = exitPos
    NEX_Cache.ExitPart   = exitPart
    print("[NEX ScanMap] Generators:", #newGens, "Gates:", #newGates, "Hooks:", #newHooks, "Windows:", #newWindows)

    local root           = Root
    if root and #NEX_Cache.Hooks > 0 then
        local closest, closestDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, hook in ipairs(NEX_Cache.Hooks) do
            if hook.part then
                local d = (hook.part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if d < closestDist then
                    closestDist = d; closest = hook
                end
            end
        end
        NEX_Cache.ClosestHook = closest
    end
end

-- MAP CHANGE DETECTION
local CurrentMapName = nil
local MapWatchConnections = {}
local MapScanQueued       = false

function DisconnectMapWatchers()
    for _, conn in ipairs(MapWatchConnections) do
        if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
    end
    MapWatchConnections = {}
end

function CheckMapChange()
    local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    local mapName = map and map.Name or "Unknown"
    if CurrentMapName ~= mapName then
        -- print("[NEX HUB] Map berubah: " .. tostring(CurrentMapName) .. " -> " .. mapName)
        VD._BeatSurvivorDone = false
        VD._BeatKillerDone = false
        VD._LastTeleAway = 0
        VD._KillerTarget = nil
    end
    CurrentMapName = mapName

    NEX_ScanMap()
end

function QueueMapScan(delaySec)
    if MapScanQueued then return end
    MapScanQueued = true
    task.delay(delaySec or 0.15, function()
        MapScanQueued = false
        if VD.Destroyed then return end
        CheckMapChange()
    end)
end

function WatchCurrentMap(map)
    DisconnectMapWatchers()
    if not map then return end

    local function onDescendantAdded(descendant)
        if descendant:IsA("Model") or descendant:IsA("Folder") then
            local n = descendant.Name:lower()
            if n:find("generator") or n:find("mesin") or n:find("pallet") or n:find("window") or n:find("hook") or n:find("gate") then
                task.delay(0.5, function()
                    if not descendant.Parent then return end
                    local primary = descendant:IsA("Model") and descendant.PrimaryPart or nil
                    local part = descendant:FindFirstChild("HitBox", true) or descendant:FindFirstChild("GeneratorPoint", true) or primary or descendant:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                    if part then
                        if n:find("generator") or n:find("mesin") then table.insert(NEX_Cache.Generators, {model=descendant, part=part})
                        elseif n:find("pallet") then table.insert(NEX_Cache.Pallets, {model=descendant, part=part})
                        elseif n:find("window") then table.insert(NEX_Cache.Windows, {model=descendant, part=part})
                        elseif n:find("hook") then table.insert(NEX_Cache.Hooks, {model=descendant, part=part})
                        elseif n:find("gate") then table.insert(NEX_Cache.Gates, {model=descendant, part=part})
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    table.insert(MapWatchConnections, map.DescendantAdded:Connect(onDescendantAdded))

    table.insert(MapWatchConnections, map.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
        if not parent then QueueMapScan(0.05) end
    end))
end

-- =====================================================
-- AUTO DODGE SPEAR (Veil)
-- =====================================================
local _lastSpearDodgeTime = 0

local function NEX_PerformSpearDodge(origin, direction, speed)
    if not VD.SURV_AutoDodgeSpear then return end
    if IsKiller(LocalPlayer) then return end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - _lastSpearDodgeTime < 1.0 then return end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char or IsDowned(char) then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end

    local speedVal = tonumber(speed) or 180
    if not direction or direction.Magnitude < 0.001 then return end
    local dirUnit = direction.Unit

    -- 1. Analisis lintasan horizontal 2D (karena gravitasi hanya menarik sumbu Y)
    local flatDir = Vector3.new(dirUnit.X, 0, dirUnit.Z)
    if flatDir.Magnitude < 0.001 then return end
    flatDir = flatDir.Unit

    local myPos = hrp.Position
    local originFlat = Vector3.new(origin.X, 0, origin.Z)
    local playerFlat = Vector3.new(myPos.X, 0, myPos.Z)
    local toPlayerFlat = playerFlat - originFlat
    local distAlongRay = toPlayerFlat:Dot(flatDir)

    -- Cek apakah tombak meluncur menuju survivor (dalam jangkauan 2 - 250 stud)
    if distAlongRay <= 2 or distAlongRay > 250 then return end

    -- Hitung titik terdekat lintasan horizontal ke survivor
    local closestFlat = originFlat + (flatDir * distAlongRay)
    local horizDist = (playerFlat - closestFlat).Magnitude

    -- Hitung perkiraan ketinggian tombak dengan efek gravitasi game (g = -98.1 stud/s^2)
    local travelTime = distAlongRay / speedVal
    local spearY = origin.Y + (dirUnit.Y * speedVal * travelTime) - (0.5 * 98.1 * travelTime * travelTime)
    local vertDiff = math.abs(myPos.Y - spearY)

    -- Cek apakah tombak menuju hitbox survivor (horizontal <= 6.5 stud dan vertikal <= 9 stud)
    if horizDist <= 6.5 and vertDiff <= 9 then
        _lastSpearDodgeTime = now

        -- 2. Tentukan vektor menghindar: SELALU menjauhi garis lintasan tombak
        local offsetFromLine = playerFlat - closestFlat
        local dodgeDir = nil

        if offsetFromLine.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            dodgeDir = offsetFromLine.Unit
        else
            -- Jika tepat sejajar di tengah, ambil arah kanan tegak lurus
            dodgeDir = Vector3.new(-flatDir.Z, 0, flatDir.X).Unit
        end

        -- 3. Jarak dodge besar (20 - 25 stud) untuk mengalahkan hit rewind & network lag
        local targetDodgeDist = 24
        local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
        rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
        rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

        -- Cek rintangan ke arah utama
        local rayMain = Workspace:Raycast(myPos, dodgeDir * (targetDodgeDist + 1.5), rayParams)
        local chosenDist = targetDodgeDist

        if rayMain then
            chosenDist = math.max(6, rayMain.Distance - 1.5)
        end

        -- Jika arah utama terhalang tembok dekat (< 10 stud), coba arah sebaliknya
        if chosenDist < 10 then
            local altDir = -dodgeDir
            local rayAlt = Workspace:Raycast(myPos, altDir * (targetDodgeDist + 1.5), rayParams)
            local altDist = rayAlt and math.max(6, rayAlt.Distance - 1.5) or targetDodgeDist
            if altDist > chosenDist then
                dodgeDir = altDir
                chosenDist = altDist
            end
        end

        -- Jika kedua sisi terhalang (koridor sempit), dodge mundur menjauhi arah tombak
        if chosenDist < 8 then
            local backDir = -flatDir
            local rayBack = Workspace:Raycast(myPos, backDir * (targetDodgeDist + 1.5), rayParams)
            local backDist = rayBack and math.max(6, rayBack.Distance - 1.5) or targetDodgeDist
            if backDist > chosenDist then
                dodgeDir = backDir
                chosenDist = backDist
            end
        end

        -- 4. Eksekusi perpindahan posisi instan + impuls physics + crouch
        pcall(function()
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + (dodgeDir * chosenDist)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = dodgeDir * 60
            task.delay(0.12, function()
                if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                end
            end)
        end)

        -- Langsung trigger crouch untuk merendahkan hitbox vertikal
        pcall(TriggerCrouch)

        pcall(VD_Notify, "Auto Dodge Spear", "Berhasil menghindar jauh dari tombak Veil!", 2)
    end
end

-- Hook 1: Direct Server Remote Listener on SpearState (0ms latency trigger)
task.spawn(function()
    local remotes = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Remotes", 5)
    local killers = remotes and remotes:WaitForChild("Killers", 5)
    local veil = killers and killers:WaitForChild("Veil", 5)
    local spearState = veil and veil:WaitForChild("SpearState", 5)
    if spearState then
        spearState.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(action, data)
            if action == "launch" and type(data) == "table" then
                if data.Source ~= LocalPlayer.Character and data.Origin and data.Direction then
                    NEX_PerformSpearDodge(data.Origin, data.Direction.Unit, data.Speed or 180)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

-- Hook 2: Workspace Projectile Listener
local function CheckSpearProjectile(child)
    if not child or not child:IsA("Model") then return end
    local name = child.Name:lower()
    local isSpear = (name == "spear" or name == "spearprojectile" or name:find("spear") ~= nil or child:GetAttribute("VeilSpearId") ~= nil)
    if not isSpear then return end

    task.spawn(function()
        if not child.Parent then return end
        local pivot = child:GetPivot()
        local origin = pivot.Position
        local direction = pivot.UpVector
        NEX_PerformSpearDodge(origin, direction, 180)
    end)
end

Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
    if child and child.Name == "Map" then
        WatchCurrentMap(child)
        QueueMapScan(0.05)
    end
    
    CheckSpearProjectile(child)
end)

Workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
    if child and child.Name == "Map" then
        DisconnectMapWatchers()
        QueueMapScan(0.05)
    end
end)

do
    local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    if map then WatchCurrentMap(map) end
    CheckMapChange()
end

-- =====================================================
-- THE BUTCHER DEFENSE (PHASE 2)
-- =====================================================
local _currentDisarmTrap = nil
local _disarmStartTick = 0
local _disarmNotified = false
local _disarmSuccessNotified = false
local _proximityTrapLaser = nil

local function NEX_UpdateTrapLaser(originPos, targetPos)
    if not _proximityTrapLaser or not _proximityTrapLaser.Parent then
        local laser = Instance.new("Part")
        laser.Name = "ButcherTrapLaser"
        laser.Anchored = true
        laser.CanCollide = false
        laser.CanTouch = false
        laser.CanQuery = false
        laser.CastShadow = false
        laser.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        laser.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 30)
        laser.Parent = workspace
        _proximityTrapLaser = laser
    end

    local dist = (targetPos - originPos).Magnitude
    _proximityTrapLaser.Size = Vector3.new(0.08, 0.08, dist)
    _proximityTrapLaser.CFrame = CFrame.new((originPos + targetPos) / 2, targetPos)
    _proximityTrapLaser.Transparency = 0
end

local function NEX_HideTrapLaser()
    if _proximityTrapLaser then
        _proximityTrapLaser.Transparency = 1
    end
end

local function NEX_HandleTrapLaserStep()
    if VD.Destroyed then
        if _proximityTrapLaser then
            pcall(function() _proximityTrapLaser:Destroy() end)
            _proximityTrapLaser = nil
        end
        return
    end

    if not VD.SURV_WarnButcherTrap then
        NEX_HideTrapLaser()
        return
    end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then
        NEX_HideTrapLaser()
        return
    end

    local nearestArmedPart = nil
    local nearestArmedDist = 35

    for _, trap in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("BearTrap")) do
        if trap and trap.Parent then
            local part = trap:IsA("BasePart") and trap or (trap:FindFirstChild("hitbox", true) or trap:FindFirstChild("HitBox", true) or trap.PrimaryPart or trap:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true))
            if part then
                local dist = (hrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                local armed = trap:GetAttribute("Armed") ~= false and trap:GetAttribute("DisarmedAt") == nil
                if armed and dist < nearestArmedDist then
                    nearestArmedDist = dist
                    nearestArmedPart = part
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if nearestArmedPart then
        local originPos = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, -1.2, 0)
        NEX_UpdateTrapLaser(originPos, nearestArmedPart.Position)
    else
        NEX_HideTrapLaser()
    end
end

local function NEX_GetButcherRemotes()
    local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local killers = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Killers")
    return killers and killers:FindFirstChild("Butcher")
end

local _trapSkillState = {
    WasActive = false,
    HasClickedThisGoal = false,
    LastTarget = nil,
    LastTargetRotation = nil,
    LastLineRotation = nil,
    LastTick = nil,
    SpawnTick = nil,
}

local function NEX_GetTrapSkillCheck()
    for _, guiName in ipairs({ "SkillCheckPromptGui", "SkillCheckPromptGui-con" }) do
        local gui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild(guiName, true)
        if gui then
            local check = gui:FindFirstChild("Check", true) or gui:FindFirstChild("check", true)
            if check and check.Visible then
                local line = check:FindFirstChild("Line", true)
                local trapPerfect = check:FindFirstChild("TrapPerfect", true)
                local trapGoal = check:FindFirstChild("TrapGoal", true)
                -- Multi-round trap struggle support:
                -- Ronde 1: TrapPerfect.Visible = true, TrapGoal.Visible = false
                -- Ronde 2+: TrapGoal.Visible = true (kotak hitam + putih), TrapPerfect.Visible = false
                local target = nil
                if trapGoal and trapGoal.Visible then
                    target = trapGoal
                elseif trapPerfect and trapPerfect.Visible then
                    target = trapPerfect
                end
                if line and target then
                    return line, target, check
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil, nil, nil
end

local function NEX_PressTrapSkill(check)
    -- 1. Direct server remote assist (terverifikasi dari log: RemoteEvent Butcher TrapSkillResult)
    pcall(function()
        local butcher = NEX_GetButcherRemotes()
        if butcher then
            local rem = butcher:FindFirstChild("TrapSkillResult")
            if rem then
                rem:FireServer("perfect")
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 2. GUI click / touch simulation (Mobile & PC)
    if check and check:IsA("GuiObject") then
        pcall(function()
            if firesignal then
                if check.MouseButton1Click then firesignal(check.MouseButton1Click) end
                if check.Activated then firesignal(check.Activated) end
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local pos = check.AbsolutePosition
            local size = check.AbsoluteSize
            local inset = GuiService:GetGuiInset()
            local x = pos.X + (size.X / 2) + inset.X
            local y = pos.Y + (size.Y / 2) + inset.Y
            VirtualInputManager:SendTouchEvent(8822, Enum.UserInputState.Begin.Value, x, y)
            task.delay(0.015, function()
                pcall(function()
                    VirtualInputManager:SendTouchEvent(8822, Enum.UserInputState.End.Value, x, y)
                end)
            end)
        end)
    end

    -- 3. Spacebar input simulation (Universal fallback)
    pcall(function()
        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
        task.delay(0.015, function()
            pcall(function()
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
            end)
        end)
    end)
end

local function NEX_HandleTrapSkillcheckStep()
    if not VD.SURV_AutoPassTrapSkill then
        _trapSkillState.WasActive = false
        _trapSkillState.HasClickedThisGoal = false
        _trapSkillState.LastTarget = nil
        _trapSkillState.LastTargetRotation = nil
        _trapSkillState.LastLineRotation = nil
        _trapSkillState.LastTick = nil
        _trapSkillState.SpawnTick = nil
        return
    end

    local line, target, check = NEX_GetTrapSkillCheck()
    if not (line and target and check) then
        _trapSkillState.WasActive = false
        _trapSkillState.HasClickedThisGoal = false
        _trapSkillState.LastTarget = nil
        _trapSkillState.LastTargetRotation = nil
        _trapSkillState.LastLineRotation = nil
        _trapSkillState.LastTick = nil
        _trapSkillState.SpawnTick = nil
        return
    end

    local lr = line.Rotation % 360
    local tr = target.Rotation % 360
    local isTrapGoal = (target.Name == "TrapGoal")

    -- Precise center offsets & zones derived from real in-game logs:
    -- TrapPerfect (Ronde 1): Center offset = +112.5 deg, Zone = [+108.0 deg, +116.0 deg]
    -- TrapGoal (Ronde 2+):    Center offset = +121.5 deg, Zone = [+117.0 deg, +126.5 deg]
    local targetOffset = isTrapGoal and 121.5 or 112.5
    local targetAngle = (tr + targetOffset) % 360
    local zoneStart = (tr + (isTrapGoal and 117.0 or 108.0)) % 360
    local zoneEnd   = (tr + (isTrapGoal and 126.5 or 116.0)) % 360
    local now = os.clock()

    if not _trapSkillState.WasActive then
        _trapSkillState.WasActive = true
        _trapSkillState.HasClickedThisGoal = false
        _trapSkillState.LastTarget = target
        _trapSkillState.LastTargetRotation = tr
        _trapSkillState.LastLineRotation = lr
        _trapSkillState.LastTick = now
        _trapSkillState.SpawnTick = now
        return
    end

    -- Jika target berganti objek atau posisi kotak berpindah (rotasi berubah > 5 derajat)
    local targetChanged = (_trapSkillState.LastTarget ~= target) or
        (_trapSkillState.LastTargetRotation and math.abs(VD_AngularDelta(_trapSkillState.LastTargetRotation, tr)) > 5)

    if targetChanged then
        _trapSkillState.HasClickedThisGoal = false
        _trapSkillState.LastLineRotation = nil
        _trapSkillState.LastTick = nil
        _trapSkillState.SpawnTick = now
    end
    _trapSkillState.LastTarget = target
    _trapSkillState.LastTargetRotation = tr

    -- Jika sudah diklik pada putaran/target ini, cek apakah jarum sudah menjauh > 50 derajat untuk reset
    if _trapSkillState.HasClickedThisGoal then
        if math.abs(VD_AngularDelta(targetAngle, lr)) > 50 then
            _trapSkillState.HasClickedThisGoal = false
        else
            _trapSkillState.LastLineRotation = lr
            _trapSkillState.LastTick = now
            return
        end
    end

    -- Delay singkat 0.05s setelah spawn/ganti ronde agar jarum dan dt terinisialisasi
    if now - (_trapSkillState.SpawnTick or now) < 0.05 then
        _trapSkillState.LastLineRotation = lr
        _trapSkillState.LastTick = now
        return
    end

    if _trapSkillState.LastLineRotation and _trapSkillState.LastTick then
        local dt = now - _trapSkillState.LastTick
        if dt > 0 and dt < 0.25 then
            local rawDelta = VD_AngularDelta(_trapSkillState.LastLineRotation, lr)
            -- Kecepatan jarum searah jarum jam (rawDelta > 0), toleransi hingga 65 derajat untuk lag spike mobile
            if rawDelta > 0 and rawDelta <= 65 then
                local inZone = VD_CrossedZone(_trapSkillState.LastLineRotation, lr, zoneStart, zoneEnd)
                local dist = math.abs(VD_AngularDelta(lr, targetAngle))

                if inZone or dist <= 4.5 then
                    _trapSkillState.HasClickedThisGoal = true
                    NEX_PressTrapSkill(check)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    _trapSkillState.LastLineRotation = lr
    _trapSkillState.LastTick = now
end

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    pcall(NEX_HandleTrapLaserStep)
    pcall(NEX_HandleTrapSkillcheckStep)
end)

local function NEX_StopDisarm(cancelled)
    if cancelled and _currentDisarmTrap then
        local targetTrap = _currentDisarmTrap:IsA("Model") and _currentDisarmTrap or (_currentDisarmTrap:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or _currentDisarmTrap)
        local butcher = NEX_GetButcherRemotes()
        local disarmRemote = (butcher and butcher:FindFirstChild("DisarmTrap")) or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DisarmTrap", true)
        if disarmRemote then
            pcall(function()
                disarmRemote:FireServer("cancel")
                if targetTrap then
                    disarmRemote:FireServer("cancel", targetTrap)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    _currentDisarmTrap = nil
    _disarmStartTick = 0
    _disarmNotified = false
    _disarmSuccessNotified = false
end

local function NEX_HandleAutoDisarmTrap()
    if not VD.SURV_AutoDisarmTrap then
        if _currentDisarmTrap then NEX_StopDisarm(true) end
        return
    end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then
        if _currentDisarmTrap then NEX_StopDisarm(true) end
        return
    end

    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then
        if _currentDisarmTrap then NEX_StopDisarm(true) end
        return
    end

    -- Jika karakter sedang terjebak di trap, jangan proses disarm
    if char:GetAttribute("Trapped") == true then
        if _currentDisarmTrap then NEX_StopDisarm(true) end
        return
    end

    local now = os.clock()
    local butcher = NEX_GetButcherRemotes()
    local disarmRemote = (butcher and butcher:FindFirstChild("DisarmTrap")) or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DisarmTrap", true)
    if not disarmRemote then return end

    -- 1. Sedang dalam proses disarm suatu trap
    if _currentDisarmTrap then
        local isGone = not _currentDisarmTrap.Parent
        local isDisarmed = isGone or (_currentDisarmTrap:GetAttribute("Armed") == false) or (_currentDisarmTrap:GetAttribute("DisarmedAt") ~= nil)

        if isDisarmed then
            if not _disarmSuccessNotified then
                _disarmSuccessNotified = true
                pcall(VD_Notify, "Auto Disarm", "Bear trap successfully disarmed!", 3)
            end
            NEX_StopDisarm()
            return
        end

        local currPart = _currentDisarmTrap:IsA("BasePart") and _currentDisarmTrap or (_currentDisarmTrap:FindFirstChild("hitbox", true) or _currentDisarmTrap:FindFirstChild("HitBox", true) or _currentDisarmTrap.PrimaryPart or _currentDisarmTrap:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true))
        local currDist = currPart and (hrp.Position - currPart.Position).Magnitude or math.huge

        -- Jika pemain menjauh melebihi jarak disarm (> 6.5 stud), batalkan
        if currDist > 6.5 then
            pcall(VD_Notify, "Auto Disarm", "Disarm cancelled (moved too far)", 2)
            NEX_StopDisarm(true)
            return
        end

        -- Pertahankan hadap karakter ke arah trap
        if currPart then
            pcall(function()
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(currPart.Position.X, hrp.Position.Y, currPart.Position.Z))
            end)
        end

        -- Selesai 5.2 detik channeling -> Commit disarm ke server
        if (now - _disarmStartTick) >= 5.2 then
            local targetTrap = _currentDisarmTrap:IsA("Model") and _currentDisarmTrap or (_currentDisarmTrap:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or _currentDisarmTrap)
            pcall(function()
                disarmRemote:FireServer("commit")
                if targetTrap then
                    disarmRemote:FireServer("commit", targetTrap)
                end
            end)
            if not _disarmSuccessNotified then
                _disarmSuccessNotified = true
                pcall(VD_Notify, "Auto Disarm", "Bear trap successfully disarmed!", 3)
            end
            NEX_StopDisarm()
            return
        end

        return
    end

    -- 2. Belum disarm: Cari trap aktif terdekat
    local nearestArmedTrap = nil
    local nearestArmedDist = math.huge
    local nearestArmedPart = nil

    for _, trap in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("BearTrap")) do
        if trap and trap.Parent then
            local part = trap:IsA("BasePart") and trap or (trap:FindFirstChild("hitbox", true) or trap:FindFirstChild("HitBox", true) or trap.PrimaryPart or trap:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true))
            if part then
                local dist = (hrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                local armed = trap:GetAttribute("Armed") ~= false and trap:GetAttribute("DisarmedAt") == nil
                if armed and dist < nearestArmedDist then
                    nearestArmedDist = dist
                    nearestArmedTrap = trap
                    nearestArmedPart = part
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Interaksi aman: antara 2.2 stud hingga 5.8 stud (HazardRadius = 1.97, DisarmRange = 6.0)
    if nearestArmedTrap and nearestArmedDist <= 5.8 and nearestArmedDist >= 2.2 then
        _currentDisarmTrap = nearestArmedTrap
        _disarmStartTick = now
        _disarmNotified = true
        _disarmSuccessNotified = false

        pcall(VD_Notify, "Auto Disarm", "Disarming bear trap... Hold position! (5s)", 3)

        local targetTrap = nearestArmedTrap:IsA("Model") and nearestArmedTrap or (nearestArmedTrap:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or nearestArmedTrap)

        -- Hadapkan karakter ke arah trap
        if nearestArmedPart then
            pcall(function()
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(nearestArmedPart.Position.X, hrp.Position.Y, nearestArmedPart.Position.Z))
            end)
        end

        -- Hentikan emote terlebih dahulu
        pcall(function()
            local eh = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes") and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes:FindFirstChild("EmoteHandler")
            if eh then
                eh:FireServer("StopEmote")
            end
        end)

        -- Panggil remote disarm begin
        pcall(function()
            disarmRemote:FireServer("begin", targetTrap)
            if targetTrap ~= nearestArmedTrap then
                disarmRemote:FireServer("begin", nearestArmedTrap)
            end
        end)

        -- Trigger mobile action button jika tersedia
        pcall(function()
            local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
            local survMob = pg and (pg:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob") or pg:FindFirstChild("Survivor", true))
            local controls = (survMob and survMob:FindFirstChild("Controls")) or (pg and pg:FindFirstChild("Controls", true))
            local actionBtn = controls and controls:FindFirstChild("action")
            if actionBtn and actionBtn:IsA("GuiButton") and actionBtn.Visible then
                if firesignal then
                    firesignal(actionBtn.MouseButton1Down)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

-- =====================================================
-- THE BUTCHER CHAIN DEFENSE (PHASE 3)
-- =====================================================
local _chainFreeing = false
local _chainStartTick = 0
local _chainSuccessNotified = false

local function NEX_StopFreeChain(cancelled)
    if cancelled and _chainFreeing then
        local butcher = NEX_GetButcherRemotes()
        local freeChainRemote = (butcher and butcher:FindFirstChild("FreeChain")) or (ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes") and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes:FindFirstChild("Killers") and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Killers:FindFirstChild("Butcher") and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Killers.Butcher:FindFirstChild("FreeChain")) or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("FreeChain", true)
        if freeChainRemote then
            pcall(function()
                freeChainRemote:FireServer("cancel")
            end)
        end
    end
    _chainFreeing = false
    _chainStartTick = 0
    _chainSuccessNotified = false
end

local function NEX_HandleAutoFreeChain()
    if not VD.SURV_AutoFreeChain then
        if _chainFreeing then NEX_StopFreeChain(true) end
        return
    end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then
        if _chainFreeing then NEX_StopFreeChain(true) end
        return
    end

    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then
        if _chainFreeing then NEX_StopFreeChain(true) end
        return
    end

    -- Cek jika karakter sedang tidak dalam kontrol bebas (downed, hooked, carried, trapped)
    if char:GetAttribute("IsDowned") or char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") or char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") or char:GetAttribute("Trapped") then
        if _chainFreeing then NEX_StopFreeChain(true) end
        return
    end

    local isChained = (char:GetAttribute("Chained") == true) or (char:GetAttribute("ChainLatched") == true) or (char:GetAttribute("ChainReeling") == true)
    local now = os.clock()
    local butcher = NEX_GetButcherRemotes()
    local freeChainRemote = (butcher and butcher:FindFirstChild("FreeChain")) or (ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes") and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes:FindFirstChild("Killers") and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Killers:FindFirstChild("Butcher") and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Killers.Butcher:FindFirstChild("FreeChain")) or ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("FreeChain", true)

    if not freeChainRemote then return end

    -- 1. Sedang dalam proses freeing rantai
    if _chainFreeing then
        -- Jika status chained sudah hilang sebelum timer selesai (rantai putus karena halangan/jarak)
        if not isChained then
            if not _chainSuccessNotified then
                _chainSuccessNotified = true
                pcall(VD_Notify, "The Butcher Chain", "Freed from chain!", 3)
            end
            NEX_StopFreeChain(false)
            return
        end

        -- Durasi standar freeing terverifikasi dari ground truth log: tepat 7 detik channeling
        if (now - _chainStartTick) >= 7.05 then
            pcall(function()
                freeChainRemote:FireServer("commit")
            end)
            if not _chainSuccessNotified then
                _chainSuccessNotified = true
                pcall(VD_Notify, "The Butcher Chain", "Successfully freed from chain!", 3)
            end
            NEX_StopFreeChain(false)
            return
        end

        return
    end

    -- 2. Belum freeing tapi karakter terjerat rantai -> Mulai Freeing
    if isChained then
        _chainFreeing = true
        _chainStartTick = now
        _chainSuccessNotified = false

        pcall(VD_Notify, "The Butcher Chain", "Freeing from chain... (Hold 7s)", 3)

        -- Hentikan emote terlebih dahulu jika ada
        pcall(function()
            local eh = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes") and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes:FindFirstChild("EmoteHandler")
            if eh then
                eh:FireServer("StopEmote")
            end
        end)

        -- Panggil remote FreeChain begin
        pcall(function()
            freeChainRemote:FireServer("begin")
        end)

        -- Trigger mobile action button jika ada
        pcall(function()
            local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
            local survMob = pg and (pg:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob") or pg:FindFirstChild("Survivor", true))
            local controls = (survMob and survMob:FindFirstChild("Controls")) or (pg and pg:FindFirstChild("Controls", true))
            local actionBtn = controls and controls:FindFirstChild("action")
            if actionBtn and actionBtn:IsA("GuiButton") and actionBtn.Visible then
                if firesignal then
                    firesignal(actionBtn.MouseButton1Down)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    while not VD.Destroyed do
        pcall(NEX_HandleAutoDisarmTrap)
        pcall(NEX_HandleAutoFreeChain)
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end)

-- =====================================================
-- AUTO DODGE BUTCHER CHAIN (V2 - PRE-EMPTIVE & HIGH-CLEARANCE)
-- =====================================================
local _lastChainDodgeTime = 0

local function NEX_PerformChainDodge(origin, direction, speed, isWindup)
    if not VD.SURV_AutoDodgeChain then return end
    if IsKiller(LocalPlayer) then return end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - _lastChainDodgeTime < 0.75 then return end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char or IsDowned(char) then return end
    if char:GetAttribute("IsDowned") or char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") or char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") or char:GetAttribute("Trapped") then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end

    local speedVal = tonumber(speed) or 140
    if not direction or direction.Magnitude < 0.001 then return end
    local dirUnit = direction.Unit

    -- 1. Analisis lintasan horizontal 2D
    local flatDir = Vector3.new(dirUnit.X, 0, dirUnit.Z)
    if flatDir.Magnitude < 0.001 then return end
    flatDir = flatDir.Unit

    local myPos = hrp.Position
    local originFlat = Vector3.new(origin.X, 0, origin.Z)
    local playerFlat = Vector3.new(myPos.X, 0, myPos.Z)
    local toPlayerFlat = playerFlat - originFlat
    local distAlongRay = toPlayerFlat:Dot(flatDir)

    -- Cek jangkauan rantai Butcher (maksimum 50 stud di game, buffer hingga 65 stud)
    if distAlongRay <= 2 or distAlongRay > 65 then return end

    -- Hitung titik terdekat lintasan horizontal ke survivor
    local closestFlat = originFlat + (flatDir * distAlongRay)
    local horizDist = (playerFlat - closestFlat).Magnitude

    -- Hitung ketinggian rantai
    local travelTime = distAlongRay / speedVal
    local chainY = origin.Y + (dirUnit.Y * speedVal * travelTime)
    local vertDiff = math.abs(myPos.Y - chainY)

    -- Ambang batas deteksi: lebih longgar pada fase windup agar menghindar lebih awal
    local maxHoriz = isWindup and 10.0 or 6.5
    local maxVert = isWindup and 9.0 or 7.5

    if horizDist <= maxHoriz and vertDiff <= maxVert then
        _lastChainDodgeTime = now

        -- 2. Tentukan arah dodge TEGAK LURUS (Perpendicular 90 derajat kiri/kanan)
        -- Jangan pernah dodge mundur di garis tembak rantai!
        local perpLeft = Vector3.new(-flatDir.Z, 0, flatDir.X).Unit
        local perpRight = -perpLeft

        local offsetFromLine = playerFlat - closestFlat
        local preferredDir = nil
        if offsetFromLine.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            preferredDir = (offsetFromLine:Dot(perpLeft) >= 0) and perpLeft or perpRight
        else
            preferredDir = perpLeft
        end
        local alternateDir = (preferredDir == perpLeft) and perpRight or perpLeft

        -- 3. Jarak dodge besar (22 - 25 stud) untuk mengalahkan client rewind & lag kompensasi killer
        local targetDodgeDist = 24
        local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
        local ignoreList = { char }
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p.Character then table.insert(ignoreList, p.Character) end
        end
        rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignoreList
        rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

        -- Elevasi titik tembak raycast ke ketinggian dada (2.5 stud di atas HRP)
        -- Ini krusial agar raycast TIDAK menabrak kontur tanah, gundukan rumput, atau tangga!
        local rayOrigin = myPos + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)

        local rayPref = Workspace:Raycast(rayOrigin, preferredDir * (targetDodgeDist + 2.0), rayParams)
        local distPref = rayPref and math.max(14, rayPref.Distance - 1.5) or targetDodgeDist

        local rayAlt = Workspace:Raycast(rayOrigin, alternateDir * (targetDodgeDist + 2.0), rayParams)
        local distAlt = rayAlt and math.max(14, rayAlt.Distance - 1.5) or targetDodgeDist

        local dodgeDir = preferredDir
        local chosenDist = distPref

        -- Pilih sisi yang paling bebas rintangan
        if distPref < 16 and distAlt > distPref then
            dodgeDir = alternateDir
            chosenDist = distAlt
        end

        -- Jika kedua sisi terhalang koridor sempit, ambil diagonal maju-samping agar tidak tertahan
        if chosenDist < 14 then
            local diagDir = (dodgeDir + flatDir).Unit
            local rayDiag = Workspace:Raycast(rayOrigin, diagDir * (targetDodgeDist + 1.5), rayParams)
            local distDiag = rayDiag and math.max(12, rayDiag.Distance - 1.5) or targetDodgeDist
            if distDiag > chosenDist then
                dodgeDir = diagDir
                chosenDist = distDiag
            end
        end

        -- 4. Eksekusi perpindahan posisi instan + impuls physics kecepatan tinggi (TANPA CROUCH slow-down)
        pcall(function()
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + (dodgeDir * chosenDist)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = dodgeDir * 75 + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
        end)

        local notifyMsg = isWindup and "Pre-emptively Dodged Chain Windup!" or "Auto Dodged Butcher Chain!"
        pcall(VD_Notify, "The Butcher Defense", notifyMsg, 2)
    end
end

-- Hook 1: Direct Server Remote Listener on ChainState (0ms server latency trigger)
task.spawn(function()
    local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes", 5)
    local killers = remotes and remotes:WaitForChild("Killers", 5)
    local butcher = killers and killers:WaitForChild("Butcher", 5)
    local chainState = butcher and butcher:WaitForChild("ChainState", 5)
    if chainState then
        chainState.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(killerInst, pivots, ropeLength, p115, p116, p117, p118, phase, extraData)
            if not VD.SURV_AutoDodgeChain then return end
            local kChar = typeof(killerInst) == "Instance" and (killerInst:IsA("Player") and killerInst.Character or killerInst) or nil
            if kChar == LocalPlayer.Character then return end

            if phase == "flying" then
                local origin = nil
                local direction = nil

                if type(extraData) == "table" and extraData.Origin and extraData.Direction then
                    origin = extraData.Origin
                    direction = extraData.Direction
                elseif type(pivots) == "table" and pivots[1] and kChar then
                    local kHead = kChar:FindFirstChild("Head") or kChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if kHead then
